struct FileBrowserState
{
    bool Open = false;
    bool RequestOpen = false;
    std::string Title = "Select File";
    std::filesystem::path CurrentDir;
    std::vector<std::string> Extensions;
    std::function<void(const std::string&)> OnSelect;
};

FileBrowserState g_Browser;

bool ExtensionMatches(const std::filesystem::path& p,
                      const std::vector<std::string>& exts)
{
    if (exts.empty()) return true;
    std::string e = PathToUtf8(p.extension());           
    std::transform(e.begin(), e.end(), e.begin(),
                   [](unsigned char c){ return (char)std::tolower(c); });
    for (const auto& x : exts)
        if (e == x) return true;
    return false;
}

ImTextureID ToImTex(uint32_t id)
{
    return reinterpret_cast<ImTextureID>(static_cast<intptr_t>(id));
}

uint32_t LoadUIIcon(const std::string& path)
{
    static std::unordered_map<std::string, uint32_t> s_Cache;

    auto it = s_Cache.find(path);
    if (it != s_Cache.end())
        return it->second;

    int w = 0, h = 0, ch = 0;
    stbi_set_flip_vertically_on_load(1);

    
    
    std::FILE* file = OpenFileUtf8(Paths::Resolve(path));
    if (!file)
    {
        BINAX_LOG_WARN("[PropertyWidgets] UI icon not found at '{}'", path);
        s_Cache[path] = 0;
        return 0;
    }
    unsigned char* data = stbi_load_from_file(file, &w, &h, &ch, 4);
    std::fclose(file);

    if (!data)
    {
        BINAX_LOG_WARN("[PropertyWidgets] UI icon load failed '{}' (stb: {})",
                       path, stbi_failure_reason());
        s_Cache[path] = 0;
        return 0;
    }

    GLuint id = 0;
    glGenTextures(1, &id);
    glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D, id);
    glTexImage2D(GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0, GL_SRGB8_ALPHA8, w, h, 0,
                 GL_RGBA, GL_UNSIGNED_BYTE, data);

    glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_S, GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
    glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_T, GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
    glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, GL_LINEAR);
    glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, GL_LINEAR);

    glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0);
    stbi_image_free(data);

    s_Cache[path] = id;
    return id;
}

bool IconButton(const char* id, uint32_t iconID, const ImVec2& size, const char* fallbackLabel)
{
    if (iconID != 0)
    {
        return ImGui::ImageButton(
            id,
            ToImTex(iconID),
            size,
            ImVec2(0, 1), ImVec2(1, 0),
            ImVec4(0, 0, 0, 0),
            ImVec4(1, 1, 1, 1));
    }
    return ImGui::Button(fallbackLabel);
}






int InputTextResizeCallback(ImGuiInputTextCallbackData* data)
{
    if (data->EventFlag == ImGuiInputTextFlags_CallbackResize)
    {
        auto* str = static_cast<std::string*>(data->UserData);
        str->resize(static_cast<size_t>(data->BufTextLen));
        data->Buf = str->data();
    }
    return 0;
}

bool InputTextString(const char* label, std::string* str,
                     ImGuiInputTextFlags flags = 0)
{
    return ImGui::InputText(label, str->data(), str->capacity() + 1,
                            flags | ImGuiInputTextFlags_CallbackResize,
                            InputTextResizeCallback, str);
}

void DrawFileBrowserPopup()
{
    if (g_Browser.RequestOpen)
    {
        ImGui::OpenPopup(g_Browser.Title.c_str());
        g_Browser.RequestOpen = false;
        g_Browser.Open = true;
    }
    if (!g_Browser.Open) return;

    ImGui::SetNextWindowSize(ImVec2(640, 420), ImGuiCond_Appearing);
    if (ImGui::BeginPopupModal(g_Browser.Title.c_str(), nullptr,
                               ImGuiWindowFlags_NoSavedSettings))
    {
        const uint32_t folderIconID = LoadUIIcon("resources/icons/folder.png");
        const uint32_t upIconID     = LoadUIIcon("resources/icons/up.png");
        const uint32_t cancelIconID = LoadUIIcon("resources/icons/cancel.png");

        const float frameH    = ImGui::GetFrameHeight();
        const ImVec2 upSize   (frameH * 1.6f, frameH);
        const ImVec2 cancelSize(frameH * 2.4f, frameH);

        
        ImGui::PushID("UpBtn");
        if (IconButton("##up", upIconID, upSize, "Up"))
        {
            auto parent = g_Browser.CurrentDir.parent_path();
            std::error_code ec;
            if (!parent.empty() && std::filesystem::exists(parent, ec) && !ec)
                g_Browser.CurrentDir = parent;
        }
        ImGui::PopID();

        ImGui::SameLine();
        ImGui::TextWrapped("%s",
            PathToUtf8(g_Browser.CurrentDir).c_str());   
        ImGui::Separator();

        
        if (ImGui::BeginChild("##listing", ImVec2(0, -32)))
        {
            const bool hasFolderIcon = (folderIconID != 0);

            std::error_code ec;
            if (std::filesystem::exists(g_Browser.CurrentDir, ec) && !ec)
            {
                std::vector<std::filesystem::path> dirs, files;
                
                
                
                
                
                try
                {
                    for (const auto& entry :
                         std::filesystem::directory_iterator(g_Browser.CurrentDir, ec))
                    {
                        if (ec) break;
                        std::error_code ec2;
                        if (entry.is_directory(ec2)) {
                            if (!ec2) dirs.push_back(entry.path());
                        } else if (entry.is_regular_file(ec2)) {
                            if (!ec2 && ExtensionMatches(entry.path(), g_Browser.Extensions))
                                files.push_back(entry.path());
                        }
                    }
                }
                catch (const std::filesystem::filesystem_error& e)
                {
                    BINAX_LOG_WARN("[PropertyWidgets] cannot list '{}': {}",
                                   PathToUtf8(g_Browser.CurrentDir), e.what());
                }
                std::sort(dirs.begin(), dirs.end());
                std::sort(files.begin(), files.end());

                const float lineH    = ImGui::GetTextLineHeight();
                const float iconSize = lineH + 4.0f;

                
                for (const auto& d : dirs)
                {
                    const std::string name = PathToUtf8(d.filename());  

                    ImGui::PushID(name.c_str());
                    ImGui::PushStyleColor(ImGuiCol_Text,
                                          ImVec4(0.25f, 0.5f, 0.9f, 1.0f));

                    if (hasFolderIcon)
                    {
                        ImGui::Image(ToImTex(folderIconID),
                                     ImVec2(iconSize, iconSize),
                                     ImVec2(0, 1), ImVec2(1, 0));
                        ImGui::SameLine();
                    }

                    const std::string label = hasFolderIcon ? name : ("[D] " + name);

                    if (ImGui::Selectable(label.c_str(), false,
                                          ImGuiSelectableFlags_AllowDoubleClick))
                    {
                        if (ImGui::IsMouseDoubleClicked(0))
                            g_Browser.CurrentDir = d;
                    }

                    ImGui::PopStyleColor();
                    ImGui::PopID();
                }

                
                for (const auto& f : files)
                {
                    const std::string name = PathToUtf8(f.filename());  
                    ImGui::PushID(name.c_str());

                    if (ImGui::Selectable(name.c_str(), false,
                                          ImGuiSelectableFlags_AllowDoubleClick))
                    {
                        if (ImGui::IsMouseDoubleClicked(0) && g_Browser.OnSelect)
                        {
                            auto cb = g_Browser.OnSelect;
                            g_Browser.OnSelect = nullptr;
                            g_Browser.Open = false;
                            ImGui::CloseCurrentPopup();
                            cb(PathToUtf8(f));   
                        }
                    }
                    ImGui::PopID();
                }
            }
            else
            {
                ImGui::TextColored(ImVec4(1, 0.4f, 0.4f, 1), "Directory not found.");
            }
            ImGui::EndChild();
        }

        
        ImGui::PushID("CancelBtn");
        if (IconButton("##cancel", cancelIconID, cancelSize, "Cancel"))
        {
            g_Browser.Open = false;
            ImGui::CloseCurrentPopup();
        }
        ImGui::PopID();

        ImGui::EndPopup();
    }
    else if (!ImGui::IsPopupOpen(g_Browser.Title.c_str()))
    {
        
        
        
        
        g_Browser.Open = false;
        g_Browser.OnSelect = nullptr;
    }
}

} 





void PropertyWidgets::OpenFileBrowser(const std::string& title,
                                      const std::vector<std::string>& extensions,
                                      std::function<void(const std::string&)> onSelect)
{
    g_Browser.Title = title;
    g_Browser.Extensions = extensions;
    g_Browser.OnSelect = std::move(onSelect);
    g_Browser.RequestOpen = true;

    if (g_Browser.CurrentDir.empty())
    {
        std::error_code ec;
        auto cwd = std::filesystem::current_path(ec);
        if (!ec) g_Browser.CurrentDir = cwd;
    }
}

void PropertyWidgets::DrawFileBrowser()
{
    DrawFileBrowserPopup();
}

bool PropertyWidgets::IsFileBrowserOpen()
{
    return g_Browser.Open || g_Browser.RequestOpen;
}

bool PropertyWidgets::Draw(void* obj, const PropertyInfo& prop)
{
    if (prop.ReadOnly) ImGui::BeginDisabled();

    bool changed = false;
    const char* label = prop.Name.c_str();

    switch (prop.Kind)
    {
    case PropertyKind::Bool:
        changed = ImGui::Checkbox(label, prop.GetPtr<bool>(obj));
        break;

    case PropertyKind::Int:
    {
        int* v = prop.GetPtr<int>(obj);
        if (prop.HasRange) changed = ImGui::SliderInt(label, v, (int)prop.Min, (int)prop.Max);
        else               changed = ImGui::DragInt  (label, v, prop.Speed);
        break;
    }
    case PropertyKind::UInt:
    {
        uint32_t* v = prop.GetPtr<uint32_t>(obj);
        changed = ImGui::DragScalar(label, ImGuiDataType_U32, v, prop.Speed);
        break;
    }
    case PropertyKind::Float:
    {
        float* v = prop.GetPtr<float>(obj);
        if (prop.HasRange) changed = ImGui::SliderFloat(label, v, prop.Min, prop.Max);
        else               changed = ImGui::DragFloat  (label, v, prop.Speed);
        break;
    }
    case PropertyKind::Double:
    {
        double* v = prop.GetPtr<double>(obj);
        float tmp = static_cast<float>(*v);
        if (ImGui::DragFloat(label, &tmp, prop.Speed))
        {
            *v = static_cast<double>(tmp);
            changed = true;
        }
        break;
    }
    case PropertyKind::Vec2:
    {
        glm::vec2* v = prop.GetPtr<glm::vec2>(obj);
        changed = ImGui::DragFloat2(label, &v->x, prop.Speed);
        break;
    }
    case PropertyKind::Vec3:
    {
        glm::vec3* v = prop.GetPtr<glm::vec3>(obj);
        changed = ImGui::DragFloat3(label, &v->x, prop.Speed);
        break;
    }
    case PropertyKind::Vec4:
    {
        glm::vec4* v = prop.GetPtr<glm::vec4>(obj);
        changed = ImGui::DragFloat4(label, &v->x, prop.Speed);
        break;
    }
    case PropertyKind::Quat:
    {
        glm::quat* q = prop.GetPtr<glm::quat>(obj);
        glm::vec3 euler = glm::degrees(glm::eulerAngles(*q));
        if (ImGui::DragFloat3(label, &euler.x, 1.0f))
        {
            *q = glm::normalize(glm::quat(glm::radians(euler)));
            changed = true;
        }
        break;
    }
    case PropertyKind::Color3:
    {
        glm::vec3* v = prop.GetPtr<glm::vec3>(obj);
        changed = ImGui::ColorEdit3(label, &v->x);
        break;
    }
    case PropertyKind::Color4:
    {
        glm::vec4* v = prop.GetPtr<glm::vec4>(obj);
        changed = ImGui::ColorEdit4(label, &v->x);
        break;
    }
    case PropertyKind::String:
    {
        std::string* s = prop.GetPtr<std::string>(obj);
        changed = InputTextString(label, s);
        break;
    }
    case PropertyKind::Enum:
    {
        
        
        
        const size_t enumSize =
            std::min<size_t>(prop.Size ? prop.Size : sizeof(int), sizeof(int));
        int value = 0;
        std::memcpy(&value, prop.GetRaw(obj), enumSize);

        std::vector<const char*> names;
        names.reserve(prop.EnumValues.size());
        int current = -1;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < prop.EnumValues.size(); ++i)
        {
            names.push_back(prop.EnumValues[i].Name.c_str());
            if (prop.EnumValues[i].Value == value) current = static_cast<int>(i);
        }

        
        
        if (!names.empty() && (current < 0 || current >= (int)names.size()))
            current = 0;

        if (!names.empty() &&
            ImGui::Combo(label, &current, names.data(), static_cast<int>(names.size())))
        {
            value = prop.EnumValues[current].Value;
            std::memcpy(prop.GetRaw(obj), &value, enumSize);
            changed = true;
        }
        break;
    }
    case PropertyKind::Asset:
    {
        std::string* path = prop.GetPtr<std::string>(obj);

        ImGui::TextUnformatted(label);
        ImGui::SameLine(170.0f);

        if (prop.AssetType == "Texture" && !path->empty())
        {
            const Texture* tex = AssetRegistry::Get().GetTexture(*path,  true);
            if (tex && tex->GetID())
            {
                float h = 28.0f;
                float w = h * (float)tex->GetWidth() / (float)tex->GetHeight();
                if (w > 48.0f) w = 48.0f;
                ImGui::Image(ToImTex(tex->GetID()), ImVec2(w, h),
                             ImVec2(0, 1), ImVec2(1, 0));
                ImGui::SameLine();
            }
        }

        
        std::string display = path->empty()
            ? "(none)"
            : PathToUtf8(PathFromUtf8(*path).filename());

        ImGui::TextUnformatted(display.c_str());
        ImGui::SameLine();

        ImGui::PushID(label);

        if (ImGui::SmallButton("..."))
        {
            std::vector<std::string> exts = { ".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg",
                                              ".tga", ".bmp", ".hdr" };
            OpenFileBrowser("Select Texture", exts,
                [path](const std::string& chosen)
                {
                    *path = chosen;
                    AssetRegistry::Get().ReloadTexture(chosen);
                });
        }

        ImGui::SameLine();
        if (ImGui::SmallButton("X"))
        {
            path->clear();
            changed = true;
        }
        ImGui::PopID();
        break;
    }
    default:
        ImGui::TextDisabled("%s (unsupported kind %d)", label, (int)prop.Kind);
        break;
    }

    if (prop.ReadOnly) ImGui::EndDisabled();
    return changed;
}

}

#pragma once

#include "Reflect/Reflection.h"

namespace Binax {

                                const std::vector<std::string>& extensions,
                                std::function<void(const std::string&)> onSelect);

================================================================================
FILE: Editor/ShaderEditor.cpp

#include "Editor/ShaderEditor.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <filesystem>
#include <fstream>
#include <sstream>

#include <imgui.h>
#include <imgui_internal.h>

#include "Renderer/im_anim.h"
#include "Utils/Log.h"
#include "Utils/Paths.h"

namespace Binax {

namespace fs = std::filesystem;

ShaderEditor::ShaderEditor()
{
    m_ShaderDir = Paths::Resolve("shaders/");
    ScanShaderDirectory();
}

void ShaderEditor::ScanShaderDirectory()
{
    m_AvailableFiles.clear();

    std::error_code ec;
    const fs::path dir = Paths::FromUtf8(m_ShaderDir);
    if (!fs::exists(dir, ec) || ec || !fs::is_directory(dir, ec))
        return;

    for (auto it = fs::directory_iterator(dir, ec);
         !ec && it != fs::directory_iterator(); it.increment(ec))
    {
        if (!it->is_regular_file()) continue;
        const std::string ext = it->path().extension().string();
        if (ext == ".vert" || ext == ".frag" || ext == ".comp"
            || ext == ".geom" || ext == ".tesc" || ext == ".tese"
            || ext == ".glsl")
        {
            m_AvailableFiles.push_back(Paths::ToUtf8(it->path()));
        }
    }

    std::sort(m_AvailableFiles.begin(), m_AvailableFiles.end());
}

bool ShaderEditor::HasFileOpen(const std::string& path) const
{
    for (const auto& s : m_Open)
        if (s.Path == path) return true;
    return false;
}

void ShaderEditor::OpenFile(const std::string& path)
{
    for (size_t i = 0; i < m_Open.size(); ++i)
    {
        if (m_Open[i].Path == path)
        {
            m_ActiveTab = (int)i;
            m_Open[i].NeedsSelect = true;
            m_Visible = true;
            return;
        }
    }

    OpenShader s;
    s.Path = path;
    s.Name = Paths::ToUtf8(Paths::FromUtf8(path).filename());
    s.NeedsSelect = true;

    s.Editor.SetLanguageDefinition(TextEditor::LanguageDefinition::GLSL());
    s.Editor.SetPalette(TextEditor::GetDarkPalette());
    s.Editor.SetShowWhitespaces(false);
    s.Editor.SetTabSize(4);
    s.Editor.SetHandleKeyboardInputs(true);
    s.Editor.SetHandleMouseInputs(true);

    LoadFileInto(s);

    m_Open.push_back(std::move(s));
    m_ActiveTab = (int)m_Open.size() - 1;
    m_Visible = true;

    BINAX_LOG_INFO("ShaderEditor: opened '{}' ({} lines)",
                   path, m_Open.back().Editor.GetTotalLines());
}

void ShaderEditor::LoadFileInto(OpenShader& s)
{
    std::ifstream file(Paths::FromUtf8(s.Path), std::ios::binary);
    if (!file.is_open())
    {
        BINAX_LOG_ERROR("ShaderEditor: cannot open '{}'", s.Path);
        s.Editor.SetText("// Failed to open file: " + s.Path);
        s.Dirty = false;
        return;
    }

    std::stringstream ss;
    ss << file.rdbuf();
    const std::string text = ss.str();

    BINAX_LOG_INFO("ShaderEditor: read '{}' ({} bytes, {} lines)",
                   s.Path, (int)text.size(),
                   (int)std::count(text.begin(), text.end(), '\n') + 1);

    s.Editor.SetText(text);
    s.Dirty = false;
}

void ShaderEditor::SaveCurrent()
{
    if (m_ActiveTab < 0 || m_ActiveTab >= (int)m_Open.size())
        return;

    OpenShader& s = m_Open[m_ActiveTab];

    std::ofstream file(Paths::FromUtf8(s.Path),
                       std::ios::binary | std::ios::trunc);
    if (!file.is_open())
    {
        BINAX_LOG_ERROR("ShaderEditor: cannot write '{}'", s.Path);
        return;
    }

    file << s.Editor.GetText();
    file.flush();
    if (!file.good())
    {
        BINAX_LOG_ERROR("ShaderEditor: write failed for '{}'", s.Path);
        return;
    }

    s.Dirty = false;
    BINAX_LOG_INFO("ShaderEditor: saved '{}'", s.Path);

    if (m_ReloadCallback)
        m_ReloadCallback(s.Path);
}

void ShaderEditor::ReloadCurrent()
{
    if (m_ActiveTab < 0 || m_ActiveTab >= (int)m_Open.size())
        return;

    OpenShader& s = m_Open[m_ActiveTab];
    LoadFileInto(s);

    if (m_ReloadCallback)
        m_ReloadCallback(s.Path);
}

void ShaderEditor::Draw()
{
    if (!m_Visible)
        return;

    m_ScanTimer += ImGui::GetIO().DeltaTime;
    if (m_ScanTimer > 2.0f)
    {
        m_ScanTimer = 0.0f;
        ScanShaderDirectory();
    }

    const float dt = ImGui::GetIO().DeltaTime;
    const float anim = iam_tween_float(
        ImHashStr("shader_editor_open"), ImHashStr("open"),
        m_Visible ? 1.0f : 0.0f,
        0.25f, iam_ease_preset(iam_ease_out_cubic),
        iam_policy_crossfade, dt);


    if (anim < 0.01f)
        return;

    ImGui::SetNextWindowSize(m_WindowSize, ImGuiCond_FirstUseEver);
    ImGui::PushStyleVar(ImGuiStyleVar_Alpha, anim);

    bool open = true;
    if (ImGui::Begin("Shader Editor", &open))
    {
        m_WindowSize = ImGui::GetWindowSize();

        if (ImGui::Button("Save"))   SaveCurrent();
        ImGui::SameLine();
        if (ImGui::Button("Reload")) ReloadCurrent();
        ImGui::SameLine();
        ImGui::TextDisabled("|");
        ImGui::SameLine();
        ImGui::TextDisabled("%d shaders · %d open",
                            (int)m_AvailableFiles.size(),
                            (int)m_Open.size());

        ImGui::Separator();

        const float listW = 220.0f;

        ImGui::BeginChild("##shaderlist", ImVec2(listW, 0), true);
        DrawFileList();
        ImGui::EndChild();

        ImGui::SameLine();

        ImGui::BeginChild("##editortabs", ImVec2(0, 0), true);
        DrawEditorTabs();
        ImGui::EndChild();
    }
    ImGui::End();
    ImGui::PopStyleVar();

    if (!open)
        m_Visible = false;
}

void ShaderEditor::DrawFileList()
{
    ImGui::TextDisabled("Shaders");
    ImGui::Separator();

    for (const auto& path : m_AvailableFiles)
    {
        const std::string name = Paths::ToUtf8(Paths::FromUtf8(path).filename());
        const bool isOpen = HasFileOpen(path);

        if (isOpen)
            ImGui::PushStyleColor(ImGuiCol_Text, ImVec4(0.5f, 0.8f, 1.0f, 1.0f));

        if (ImGui::Selectable(name.c_str(), false))
            OpenFile(path);

        if (isOpen)
            ImGui::PopStyleColor();

        if (ImGui::IsItemHovered())
            ImGui::SetTooltip("%s", path.c_str());
    }

    if (m_AvailableFiles.empty())
        ImGui::TextDisabled("(no shader files found in shaders/)");
}

void ShaderEditor::DrawEditorTabs()
{
    if (m_Open.empty())
    {
        ImGui::TextDisabled("Open a shader file from the list on the left.");
        return;
    }

    if (!ImGui::BeginTabBar("##shadertabs", ImGuiTabBarFlags_Reorderable))
    {


        return;
    }

    bool anyTabDrawn = false;

    for (int i = 0; i < (int)m_Open.size(); ++i)
    {
        OpenShader& s = m_Open[i];
        std::string label = s.Name;
        if (s.Dirty) label += " *";

        ImGuiTabItemFlags flags = 0;
        if (s.NeedsSelect)
            flags |= ImGuiTabItemFlags_SetSelected;

        bool wantClose = false;
        if (ImGui::BeginTabItem(label.c_str(), &wantClose, flags))
        {
            anyTabDrawn = true;
            s.NeedsSelect = false;
            m_ActiveTab = i;

            ImGui::TextDisabled("%s", s.Path.c_str());
            ImGui::Separator();


            ImVec2 avail = ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail();
            if (avail.x < 100.0f) avail.x = 400.0f;
            if (avail.y < 100.0f) avail.y = 300.0f;

            s.Editor.Render("##editor", avail, false);

            if (s.Editor.IsTextChanged())
                s.Dirty = true;

            ImGui::EndTabItem();
        }

        if (wantClose)
        {
            m_Open.erase(m_Open.begin() + i);
            if (m_ActiveTab >= (int)m_Open.size())
                m_ActiveTab = (int)m_Open.size() - 1;
            ImGui::EndTabBar();
            return;
        }
    }



    if (!anyTabDrawn && m_ActiveTab >= 0 && m_ActiveTab < (int)m_Open.size())
    {
        OpenShader& s = m_Open[m_ActiveTab];
        ImGui::TextDisabled("%s", s.Path.c_str());
        ImGui::Separator();

        ImVec2 avail = ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail();
        if (avail.x < 100.0f) avail.x = 400.0f;
        if (avail.y < 100.0f) avail.y = 300.0f;

        s.Editor.Render("##editor_fallback", avail, false);
    }

    ImGui::EndTabBar();
}

}

================================================================================
FILE: Editor/ShaderEditor.h
================================================================================
#pragma once

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <functional>
#include <imgui.h>

#include "Editor/TextEditor.h"

namespace Binax {

class ShaderEditor
{
public:
    ShaderEditor();


    void SetReloadCallback(std::function<void(const std::string&)> cb)
    {
        m_ReloadCallback = std::move(cb);
    }

    void SetVisible(bool v) { m_Visible = v; }
    bool IsVisible() const  { return m_Visible; }
    void ToggleVisible()    { m_Visible = !m_Visible; }


    void OpenFile(const std::string& path);


    void Draw();

private:
    struct OpenShader
    {
        std::string Path;
        std::string Name;
        TextEditor  Editor;
        bool        Dirty = false;
        bool        NeedsSelect = false;
    };

    void ScanShaderDirectory();
    void LoadFileInto(OpenShader& s);
    void SaveCurrent();
    void ReloadCurrent();
    bool HasFileOpen(const std::string& path) const;

    void DrawFileList();
    void DrawEditorTabs();

    std::vector<OpenShader> m_Open;
    int                     m_ActiveTab = 0;
    bool                    m_Visible   = false;

    std::string              m_ShaderDir;
    std::vector<std::string> m_AvailableFiles;
    float                    m_ScanTimer = 0.0f;

    std::function<void(const std::string&)> m_ReloadCallback;

    ImVec2 m_WindowSize{900.0f, 560.0f};
};

}

================================================================================
FILE: Editor/Viewport.cpp
================================================================================
#include "Viewport.h"
#include <glad/glad.h>
#include "../Utils/Log.h"

namespace Binax {

Viewport::Viewport(uint32_t width, uint32_t height)
    : m_Width(width), m_Height(height)
{
    glGenFramebuffers(1, &m_FBO);
    glBindFramebuffer(GL_FRAMEBUFFER, m_FBO);

    glGenTextures(1, &m_TextureID);
    glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D, m_TextureID);

    
    
    glTexImage2D(GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0, GL_RGBA8,
                 m_Width, m_Height, 0,
                 GL_RGBA, GL_UNSIGNED_BYTE, nullptr);
    glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, GL_LINEAR);
    glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, GL_LINEAR);
    glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_S, GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
    glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_T, GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
    glFramebufferTexture2D(GL_FRAMEBUFFER, GL_COLOR_ATTACHMENT0,
                           GL_TEXTURE_2D, m_TextureID, 0);

    glGenRenderbuffers(1, &m_RBO);
    glBindRenderbuffer(GL_RENDERBUFFER, m_RBO);
    glRenderbufferStorage(GL_RENDERBUFFER, GL_DEPTH24_STENCIL8, m_Width, m_Height);
    glFramebufferRenderbuffer(GL_FRAMEBUFFER, GL_DEPTH_STENCIL_ATTACHMENT,
                              GL_RENDERBUFFER, m_RBO);

    if (glCheckFramebufferStatus(GL_FRAMEBUFFER) != GL_FRAMEBUFFER_COMPLETE)
        BINAX_LOG_ERROR("Viewport FBO incomplete!");

    glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0);
    glBindRenderbuffer(GL_RENDERBUFFER, 0);
    glBindFramebuffer(GL_FRAMEBUFFER, 0);
}

Viewport::~Viewport()
{
    glDeleteFramebuffers(1, &m_FBO);
    glDeleteTextures(1, &m_TextureID);
    glDeleteRenderbuffers(1, &m_RBO);
}

void Viewport::Resize(uint32_t width, uint32_t height)
{
    if (width == 0 || height == 0) return;
    if (width == m_Width && height == m_Height) return;

    m_Width = width;
    m_Height = height;

    
    
    GLint prevFBO = 0;
    glGetIntegerv(GL_FRAMEBUFFER_BINDING, &prevFBO);
    GLint prevTex = 0;
    glGetIntegerv(GL_TEXTURE_BINDING_2D, &prevTex);

    glBindFramebuffer(GL_FRAMEBUFFER, m_FBO);
    glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D, m_TextureID);
    glTexImage2D(GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0, GL_RGBA8,
                 m_Width, m_Height, 0,
                 GL_RGBA, GL_UNSIGNED_BYTE, nullptr);
    glBindRenderbuffer(GL_RENDERBUFFER, m_RBO);
    glRenderbufferStorage(GL_RENDERBUFFER, GL_DEPTH24_STENCIL8, m_Width, m_Height);

    glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D, static_cast<GLuint>(prevTex));
    glBindRenderbuffer(GL_RENDERBUFFER, 0);
    glBindFramebuffer(GL_FRAMEBUFFER, static_cast<GLuint>(prevFBO));
}

void Viewport::Bind() const
{
    glBindFramebuffer(GL_FRAMEBUFFER, m_FBO);
    glViewport(0, 0, m_Width, m_Height);
}

void Viewport::Unbind() const
{
    glBindFramebuffer(GL_FRAMEBUFFER, 0);
}

}

================================================================================
FILE: Editor/Viewport.h
================================================================================

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <glm/glm.hpp>

namespace Binax {

class Viewport
{
public:
    Viewport(uint32_t width, uint32_t height);
    ~Viewport();

    void Resize(uint32_t width, uint32_t height);
    void Bind() const;
    void Unbind() const;
    uint32_t GetTextureID() const { return m_TextureID; }
    uint32_t GetFBO() const { return m_FBO; }
    glm::vec2 GetSize() const { return glm::vec2(m_Width, m_Height); }
    float GetAspect() const { return m_Height > 0 ? static_cast<float>(m_Width) / static_cast<float>(m_Height) : 1.0f; }

private:
    uint32_t m_FBO;
    uint32_t m_TextureID;
    uint32_t m_RBO;
    uint32_t m_Width, m_Height;
};

}

FILE: Reflect/ComponentRegistry.cpp

#include "Reflect/ComponentRegistry.h"
#include "Utils/Log.h"

namespace Binax::Reflect {

ComponentRegistry& ComponentRegistry::Get()
{
    static ComponentRegistry instance;
    return instance;
}

void ComponentRegistry::WarnDuplicate(const std::string& name) const
{
    BINAX_LOG_WARN("Component '{}' already registered - ignoring", name);
}

const ComponentRegistry::Entry* ComponentRegistry::Find(std::string_view name) const
{
    auto it = m_ByName.find(std::string(name));
    return it != m_ByName.end() ? m_Entries[it->second].get() : nullptr;
}

const ComponentRegistry::Entry* ComponentRegistry::Find(const void* typeId) const
{
    auto it = m_ByTypeId.find(typeId);
    return it != m_ByTypeId.end() ? m_Entries[it->second].get() : nullptr;
}

}

================================================================================
FILE: Reflect/ComponentRegistry.h
================================================================================
#pragma once

#include "Reflect/Reflection.h"

namespace Binax::Reflect {

class ComponentRegistry
{
public:
    struct Entry
    {
        std::string Name;               
        const void* TypeId = nullptr;

        
        
        bool Hidden    = false;
        bool Transient = false;

        std::function<bool(entt::registry&, entt::entity)> Has;
        std::function<void(entt::registry&, entt::entity)> AddDefault;
        std::function<void(entt::registry&, entt::entity)> Remove;
        std::function<void*(entt::registry&, entt::entity)> GetPtr;
        std::function<const void*(const entt::registry&, entt::entity)> GetPtrConst;
    };

    static ComponentRegistry& Get();

    template<typename T>
    void Register(std::string uiName, bool hidden = false, bool transient = false);

    const Entry* Find(std::string_view name) const;
    const Entry* Find(const void* typeId) const;

    const std::vector<std::unique_ptr<Entry>>& All() const { return m_Entries; }

private:
    ComponentRegistry() = default;
    ComponentRegistry(const ComponentRegistry&) = delete;
    ComponentRegistry& operator=(const ComponentRegistry&) = delete;

    
    
    void WarnDuplicate(const std::string& name) const;

    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<Entry>> m_Entries;
    std::unordered_map<std::string, size_t> m_ByName;
    std::unordered_map<const void*, size_t> m_ByTypeId;
};

template<typename T>
void ComponentRegistry::Register(std::string uiName, bool hidden, bool transient)
{
    
    
    
    if (m_ByName.count(uiName) || m_ByTypeId.count(GetTypeId<T>()))
    {
        WarnDuplicate(uiName);
        return;
    }

    auto e = std::make_unique<Entry>();
    e->Name      = std::move(uiName);
    e->TypeId    = GetTypeId<T>();
    e->Hidden    = hidden;
    e->Transient = transient;

    e->Has        = [](entt::registry& r, entt::entity en) { return r.any_of<T>(en); };
    e->AddDefault = [](entt::registry& r, entt::entity en) { if (!r.any_of<T>(en)) r.emplace<T>(en); };
    e->Remove     = [](entt::registry& r, entt::entity en) { if (r.any_of<T>(en))  r.remove<T>(en); };
    e->GetPtr     = [](entt::registry& r, entt::entity en) -> void* {
        return r.any_of<T>(en) ? static_cast<void*>(&r.get<T>(en)) : nullptr;
    };
    e->GetPtrConst = [](const entt::registry& r, entt::entity en) -> const void* {
        return r.any_of<T>(en) ? static_cast<const void*>(&r.get<T>(en)) : nullptr;
    };

    size_t idx = m_Entries.size();
    m_ByName[e->Name]     = idx;
    m_ByTypeId[e->TypeId] = idx;
    m_Entries.push_back(std::move(e));
}

}

namespace Binax::Reflect {

void RegisterEngineComponents()
{
    static bool done = false;
    if (done) return;
    done = true;

    auto& cr = ComponentRegistry::Get();
    cr.Register<Transform>             ("Transform");
    cr.Register<MeshComponent>         ("Mesh Component");
    cr.Register<MaterialComponent>     ("Material");
    cr.Register<LightComponent>        ("Light Component");
    cr.Register<CameraComponent>       ("Camera Component");
    cr.Register<NameComponent>         ("Name",                  true,  false);
    cr.Register<EditorCameraComponent> ("EditorCameraComponent", true,  true);
    cr.Register<SkyboxComponent>       ("Skybox");
    cr.Register<PostProcessComponent>  ("Post Processing");
}

}

================================================================================
FILE: Reflect/Reflection.cpp
================================================================================

#include "Reflect/Reflection.h"
#include "Utils/Log.h"

namespace Binax::Reflect {

TypeRegistry& TypeRegistry::Get()
{
    static TypeRegistry instance;
    return instance;
}

void TypeRegistry::Register(const void* typeId, ClassInfo info)
{
    if (m_Types.count(typeId))
        BINAX_LOG_WARN("Type '{}' already registered — overwriting", info.Name);

    m_ByName[info.Name] = typeId;
    m_Types[typeId]     = std::move(info);
}

const ClassInfo* TypeRegistry::Find(const void* typeId) const
{
    auto it = m_Types.find(typeId);
    return it != m_Types.end() ? &it->second : nullptr;
}

const ClassInfo* TypeRegistry::FindByName(std::string_view name) const
{
    auto it = m_ByName.find(std::string(name));
    if (it == m_ByName.end()) return nullptr;
    return Find(it->second);
}

}

================================================================================
FILE: Reflect/Reflection.h
================================================================================

#pragma once

#include <entt/entt.hpp>
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <glm/gtc/quaternion.hpp>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstring>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <functional>
#include <memory>

namespace Binax::Reflect {

template<typename T>
struct TypeIdHolder { static const char Id; };

template<typename T>
const char TypeIdHolder<T>::Id = 0;

template<typename T>
inline const void* GetTypeId() noexcept { return &TypeIdHolder<T>::Id; }

enum class PropertyKind : uint8_t
{
    Bool, Int, UInt, Float, Double,
    Vec2, Vec3, Vec4, Quat,
    Color3, Color4,
    String,
    Enum,
    Entity,
    Asset,
    Custom
};

struct EnumEntry
{
    int Value = 0;
    std::string Name;
};

struct PropertyInfo
{
    std::string Name;
    PropertyKind Kind = PropertyKind::Custom;
    uint32_t Offset = 0;
    uint32_t Size   = 0;

    float Min = 0.0f;
    float Max = 0.0f;
    float Speed = 0.1f;
    bool  HasRange = false;

    bool  ReadOnly  = false;
    bool  Transient = false;

    
    std::string AssetType;

    std::vector<EnumEntry> EnumValues;

    template<typename T> T*       GetPtr(void* obj)       const noexcept
    { return reinterpret_cast<T*>(static_cast<char*>(obj) + Offset); }
    template<typename T> const T* GetPtr(const void* obj) const noexcept
    { return reinterpret_cast<const T*>(static_cast<const char*>(obj) + Offset); }

    
    
    void*       GetRaw(void* obj)       const noexcept
    { return static_cast<char*>(obj) + Offset; }
    const void* GetRaw(const void* obj) const noexcept
    { return static_cast<const char*>(obj) + Offset; }

    PropertyInfo& SetSpeed(float s)                  { Speed = s; return *this; }
    PropertyInfo& SetRange(float mn, float mx)       { Min = mn; Max = mx; HasRange = true; return *this; }
    PropertyInfo& SetEnums(std::vector<EnumEntry> e) { EnumValues = std::move(e); return *this; }
    PropertyInfo& SetReadOnly()                      { ReadOnly  = true; return *this; }
    PropertyInfo& SetTransient()                     { Transient = true; return *this; }
    PropertyInfo& SetAssetType(std::string t)        { AssetType = std::move(t); return *this; }
};

struct ClassInfo
{
    std::string Name;
    std::string DisplayName;
    size_t Size = 0;
    std::vector<PropertyInfo> Properties;

    template<typename C, typename M>
    PropertyInfo& Add(M C::* member, std::string name, PropertyKind kind)
    {
        PropertyInfo p;
        p.Name   = std::move(name);
        p.Kind   = kind;
        p.Size   = static_cast<uint32_t>(sizeof(M));
        p.Offset = MemberOffset(member);
        Properties.push_back(std::move(p));
        return Properties.back();
    }

    const PropertyInfo* Find(std::string_view name) const
    {
        for (const auto& p : Properties)
            if (p.Name == name) return &p;
        return nullptr;
    }

private:
    template<typename C, typename M>
    static uint32_t MemberOffset(M C::* member) noexcept
    {
        constexpr uintptr_t kFake = 0x1000;
        const C* base = reinterpret_cast<const C*>(kFake);
        const M* m    = &(base->*member);
        return static_cast<uint32_t>(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(m)
                                     - reinterpret_cast<const char*>(base));
    }
};

class TypeRegistry
{
public:
    static TypeRegistry& Get();

    void Register(const void* typeId, ClassInfo info);
    const ClassInfo* Find(const void* typeId) const;
    const ClassInfo* FindByName(std::string_view name) const;

    template<typename T>
    const ClassInfo* Find() const { return Find(GetTypeId<T>()); }

    const std::unordered_map<const void*, ClassInfo>& All() const { return m_Types; }

private:
    TypeRegistry() = default;
    TypeRegistry(const TypeRegistry&) = delete;
    TypeRegistry& operator=(const TypeRegistry&) = delete;

    std::unordered_map<const void*, ClassInfo> m_Types;
    std::unordered_map<std::string, const void*> m_ByName;
};

template<typename T>
struct TypeRegistrar;

} 









#define BINAX_REFLECT_CONCAT_(a, b) a##b
#define BINAX_REFLECT_CONCAT(a, b)  BINAX_REFLECT_CONCAT_(a, b)

#define BINAX_REFLECT(Type)                                                      \
    namespace Binax::Reflect {                                                   \
        template<> struct TypeRegistrar<Type> {                                  \
            static void Register(ClassInfo& info);                               \
        };                                                                       \
    }                                                                            \
    namespace {                                                                  \
        [[maybe_unused]]                                                         \
        const bool BINAX_REFLECT_CONCAT(g_ReflectReg_, __LINE__) = []() {        \
            ::Binax::Reflect::ClassInfo info_;                                   \
            std::string full_ = #Type;                                           \
            auto pos_ = full_.rfind("::");                                       \
            info_.DisplayName = (pos_ == std::string::npos)                      \
                                ? full_ : full_.substr(pos_ + 2);                \
            info_.Name = std::move(full_);                                       \
            info_.Size = sizeof(Type);                                           \
            ::Binax::Reflect::TypeRegistrar<Type>::Register(info_);              \
            ::Binax::Reflect::TypeRegistry::Get().Register(                      \
                ::Binax::Reflect::GetTypeId<Type>(), std::move(info_));          \
            return true;                                                         \
        }();                                                                     \
    }                                                                            \
    void Binax::Reflect::TypeRegistrar<Type>::Register(Binax::Reflect::ClassInfo& info)

================================================================================
FILE: Renderer/AssetRegistry.cpp
================================================================================
#include "AssetRegistry.h"
#include "Utils/Log.h"

#include <glad/glad.h>

namespace Binax {

AssetRegistry& AssetRegistry::Get()
{
    static AssetRegistry instance;
    return instance;
}



std::string AssetRegistry::MakeTextureKey(const std::string& path, bool srgb)
{
    return path + (srgb ? "|sRGB" : "|Linear");
}

const Texture* AssetRegistry::GetTexture(const std::string& path, bool srgb)
{
    if (path.empty())
        return nullptr;

    const std::string key = MakeTextureKey(path, srgb);

    if (m_Failed.count(key))
        return nullptr;

    auto it = m_Textures.find(key);
    if (it != m_Textures.end())
        return it->second.get();

    auto tex = Texture::LoadFromFile(path, srgb);
    if (!tex)
    {
        m_Failed.insert(key);
        return nullptr;
    }

    const Texture* raw = tex.get();
    m_Textures[key] = std::move(tex);
    BINAX_LOG_INFO("AssetRegistry: loaded texture '{}'", path);
    return raw;
}

TextureHandle AssetRegistry::GetTextureHandle(const std::string& path, bool srgb)
{
    if (path.empty())
        return kInvalidTextureHandle;

    const std::string key = MakeTextureKey(path, srgb);

    auto it = m_Handles.find(key);
    if (it != m_Handles.end())
        return it->second;

    if (!GLAD_GL_ARB_bindless_texture)
    {
        BINAX_LOG_WARN("AssetRegistry: bindless texture unsupported — "
                       "texture '{}' will be ignored", path);
        m_Handles[key] = kInvalidTextureHandle;
        return kInvalidTextureHandle;
    }

    const Texture* tex = GetTexture(path, srgb);
    if (!tex || !tex->GetID())
    {
        m_Handles[key] = kInvalidTextureHandle;
        return kInvalidTextureHandle;
    }

    GLuint64 h = glGetTextureHandleARB(tex->GetID());
    if (h != 0 && !glIsTextureHandleResidentARB(h))
        glMakeTextureHandleResidentARB(h);

    m_Handles[key] = (TextureHandle)h;
    BINAX_LOG_INFO("AssetRegistry: bindless handle for '{}' = {}", path,
                   (unsigned long long)h);
    return m_Handles[key];
}

void AssetRegistry::ReloadTexture(const std::string& path)
{
    if (path.empty())
        return;

    
    
    for (const bool srgb : { true, false })
    {
        const std::string key = MakeTextureKey(path, srgb);

        auto hit = m_Handles.find(key);
        if (hit != m_Handles.end())
        {
            if (hit->second != kInvalidTextureHandle && GLAD_GL_ARB_bindless_texture)
            {
                GLuint64 h = (GLuint64)hit->second;
                if (glIsTextureHandleResidentARB(h))
                    glMakeTextureHandleNonResidentARB(h);
            }
            m_Handles.erase(hit);
        }

        m_Failed.erase(key);
        m_Textures.erase(key);
    }
}



std::string AssetRegistry::MakeCubemapKey(const std::array<std::string, 6>& paths)
{
    std::string key;
    for (const auto& p : paths)
    {
        key += p;
        key += '|';
    }
    return key;
}

const Cubemap* AssetRegistry::GetCubemap(const std::array<std::string, 6>& paths, bool srgb)
{
    for (const auto& p : paths)
        if (p.empty())
            return nullptr;

    const std::string key = MakeCubemapKey(paths);

    if (m_FailedCubemaps.count(key))
        return nullptr;

    auto it = m_Cubemaps.find(key);
    if (it != m_Cubemaps.end())
        return it->second.get();

    auto cube = Cubemap::LoadFromFiles(paths, srgb);
    if (!cube)
    {
        m_FailedCubemaps.insert(key);
        return nullptr;
    }

    const Cubemap* raw = cube.get();
    m_Cubemaps[key] = std::move(cube);
    BINAX_LOG_INFO("AssetRegistry: loaded cubemap ({} px)", raw->GetSize());
    return raw;
}

void AssetRegistry::ReloadCubemap(const std::array<std::string, 6>& paths)
{
    const std::string key = MakeCubemapKey(paths);
    m_FailedCubemaps.erase(key);
    m_Cubemaps.erase(key);
}

void AssetRegistry::Clear()
{
    
    if (GLAD_GL_ARB_bindless_texture)
    {
        for (auto& [path, h] : m_Handles)
        {
            if (h != kInvalidTextureHandle)
            {
                GLuint64 raw = (GLuint64)h;
                if (glIsTextureHandleResidentARB(raw))
                    glMakeTextureHandleNonResidentARB(raw);
            }
        }
    }
    m_Handles.clear();

    m_Textures.clear();
    m_Failed.clear();

    m_Cubemaps.clear();
    m_FailedCubemaps.clear();
}

}

================================================================================
FILE: Renderer/AssetRegistry.h
================================================================================
#pragma once

#include "Texture.h"
#include "Cubemap.h"
#include "Renderer/TextureHandle.h"
#include <array>
#include <memory>
#include <string>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <unordered_set>

namespace Binax {

class AssetRegistry
{
public:
    static AssetRegistry& Get();

    
    const Texture* GetTexture(const std::string& path, bool srgb = true);

    
    TextureHandle GetTextureHandle(const std::string& path, bool srgb = true);

    void ReloadTexture(const std::string& path);

    
    const Cubemap* GetCubemap(const std::array<std::string, 6>& paths, bool srgb = true);
    void ReloadCubemap(const std::array<std::string, 6>& paths);

    void Clear();

private:
    AssetRegistry() = default;

    
    
    
    
    static std::string MakeTextureKey(const std::string& path, bool srgb);

    static std::string MakeCubemapKey(const std::array<std::string, 6>& paths);

    std::unordered_map<std::string, std::unique_ptr<Texture>> m_Textures;
    std::unordered_set<std::string> m_Failed;

    std::unordered_map<std::string, TextureHandle> m_Handles;

    std::unordered_map<std::string, std::unique_ptr<Cubemap>> m_Cubemaps;
    std::unordered_set<std::string> m_FailedCubemaps;
};

}

================================================================================
FILE: Renderer/Cubemap.cpp
================================================================================
#include "Cubemap.h"
#include "Utils/Log.h"
#include "Utils/FileIO.h"

#include <glad/glad.h>
#include <stb/stb_image.h>

namespace Binax {

Cubemap::~Cubemap()
{
    if (m_ID) glDeleteTextures(1, &m_ID);
}

Cubemap::Cubemap(Cubemap&& other) noexcept
    : m_ID(other.m_ID), m_Size(other.m_Size)
{
    other.m_ID = 0;
}

Cubemap& Cubemap::operator=(Cubemap&& other) noexcept
{
    if (this != &other)
    {
        if (m_ID) glDeleteTextures(1, &m_ID);
        m_ID       = other.m_ID;
        m_Size     = other.m_Size;
        other.m_ID = 0;
    }
    return *this;
}

std::unique_ptr<Cubemap> Cubemap::LoadFromFiles(const std::array<std::string, 6>& paths, bool srgb)
{
    stbi_set_flip_vertically_on_load(0);

    auto cube = std::unique_ptr<Cubemap>(new Cubemap());
    glGenTextures(1, &cube->m_ID);
    glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_CUBE_MAP, cube->m_ID);

    int faceSize = 0;

    for (int i = 0; i < 6; ++i)
    {
        if (paths[i].empty())
        {
            BINAX_LOG_ERROR("Cubemap face {} path is empty", i);
            glDeleteTextures(1, &cube->m_ID);
            cube->m_ID = 0;
            return nullptr;
        }

        
        std::FILE* file = OpenFileUtf8(paths[i]);
        if (!file)
        {
            BINAX_LOG_ERROR("Cubemap face {}: cannot open '{}'", i, paths[i]);
            glDeleteTextures(1, &cube->m_ID);
            cube->m_ID = 0;
            return nullptr;
        }

        int w = 0, h = 0, ch = 0;
        unsigned char* data = stbi_load_from_file(file, &w, &h, &ch, 0);
        std::fclose(file);

        if (!data)
        {
            BINAX_LOG_ERROR("Cubemap face {} load failed '{}': {}",
                            i, paths[i], stbi_failure_reason());
            glDeleteTextures(1, &cube->m_ID);
            cube->m_ID = 0;
            return nullptr;
        }

        if (w != h)
        {
            BINAX_LOG_ERROR("Cubemap face {} not square ({}x{})", i, w, h);
            stbi_image_free(data);
            glDeleteTextures(1, &cube->m_ID);
            cube->m_ID = 0;
            return nullptr;
        }

        if (faceSize == 0)
            faceSize = w;
        else if (w != faceSize)
        {
            
            
            BINAX_LOG_ERROR("Cubemap face {} size {}x{} != first face size {}x{}",
                            i, w, h, faceSize, faceSize);
            stbi_image_free(data);
            glDeleteTextures(1, &cube->m_ID);
            cube->m_ID = 0;
            return nullptr;
        }

        GLenum internalFmt = GL_RGB8;
        GLenum fmt         = GL_RGB;
        
        
        if (ch == 1)      { internalFmt = GL_R8;  fmt = GL_RED; }
        else if (ch == 2) { internalFmt = GL_RG8; fmt = GL_RG;  }
        else if (ch == 3) { internalFmt = srgb ? GL_SRGB8        : GL_RGB8;  fmt = GL_RGB;  }
        else if (ch == 4) { internalFmt = srgb ? GL_SRGB8_ALPHA8 : GL_RGBA8; fmt = GL_RGBA; }

        glTexImage2D(GL_TEXTURE_CUBE_MAP_POSITIVE_X + i, 0,
                     internalFmt, w, h, 0, fmt, GL_UNSIGNED_BYTE, data);
        stbi_image_free(data);
    }

    glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_CUBE_MAP, GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_S, GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
    glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_CUBE_MAP, GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_T, GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
    glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_CUBE_MAP, GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_R, GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
    glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_CUBE_MAP, GL_TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, GL_LINEAR_MIPMAP_LINEAR);
    glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_CUBE_MAP, GL_TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, GL_LINEAR);
    glGenerateMipmap(GL_TEXTURE_CUBE_MAP);

#ifdef GL_TEXTURE_MAX_ANISOTROPY_EXT
    if (GLAD_GL_EXT_texture_filter_anisotropic)
    {
        GLfloat maxAniso = 1.0f;
        glGetFloatv(GL_MAX_TEXTURE_MAX_ANISOTROPY_EXT, &maxAniso);
        if (maxAniso > 1.0f)
            glTexParameterf(GL_TEXTURE_CUBE_MAP, GL_TEXTURE_MAX_ANISOTROPY_EXT, maxAniso);
    }
#endif

    glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_CUBE_MAP, 0);
    cube->m_Size = faceSize;
    return cube;
}

void Cubemap::Bind(uint32_t slot) const
{
    glActiveTexture(GL_TEXTURE0 + slot);
    glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_CUBE_MAP, m_ID);
}

void Cubemap::Unbind(uint32_t slot) const
{
    glActiveTexture(GL_TEXTURE0 + slot);
    glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_CUBE_MAP, 0);
}

}

    void Bind(uint32_t slot = 0) const;
    void Unbind(uint32_t slot = 0) const;

#include "Renderer/DeferredRenderer.h"
#include "Renderer/AssetRegistry.h"
#include "Renderer/Mesh.h"
#include "Renderer/MeshLibrary.h"
#include "Renderer/InstanceBuffer.h"
#include "Utils/Log.h"
