================================================================================
FILE: Renderer/MeshLibrary.cpp
================================================================================
#include "Renderer/MeshLibrary.h"
#include "Utils/Log.h"

#include <glad/glad.h>
#include <algorithm>

namespace Binax {

MeshLibrary& MeshLibrary::Get()
{
    static MeshLibrary instance;
    return instance;
}

MeshHandle MeshLibrary::Add(const std::string& name,
                            const std::vector<Vertex>& vertices,
                            const std::vector<uint32_t>& indices)
{
    auto it = m_ByName.find(name);
    if (it != m_ByName.end())
        return it->second;

    if (vertices.empty() || indices.empty())
    {
        BINAX_LOG_WARN("MeshLibrary::Add('{}') — empty mesh", name);
        return kInvalidMesh;
    }

    MeshGPU gpu;
    gpu.BaseVertex  = (uint32_t)m_CPUVertices.size();
    gpu.VertexCount = (uint32_t)vertices.size();
    gpu.BaseIndex   = (uint32_t)m_CPUIndices.size();
    gpu.IndexCount  = (uint32_t)indices.size();

    glm::vec3 mn = vertices[0].Position;
    glm::vec3 mx = vertices[0].Position;
    for (const auto& v : vertices)
    {
        mn = glm::min(mn, v.Position);
        mx = glm::max(mx, v.Position);
    }
    gpu.AABBMin = mn;
    gpu.AABBMax = mx;

    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    m_CPUVertices.insert(m_CPUVertices.end(), vertices.begin(), vertices.end());
    m_CPUIndices.insert(m_CPUIndices.end(), indices.begin(), indices.end());

    MeshHandle h = (MeshHandle)m_Meshes.size();
    m_Meshes.push_back(gpu);
    m_Names.push_back(name);
    m_ByName[name] = h;
    m_Dirty = true;

    BINAX_LOG_INFO("MeshLibrary: added '{}' (verts={}, idx={})",
                   name, gpu.VertexCount, gpu.IndexCount);
    return h;
}

MeshHandle MeshLibrary::Find(const std::string& name) const
{
    auto it = m_ByName.find(name);
    return it == m_ByName.end() ? kInvalidMesh : it->second;
}

void MeshLibrary::BindVAO() const
{
    glBindVertexArray(m_VAO);
}

void MeshLibrary::Flush()
{
    if (!m_Dirty && m_VAOReady) return;

    
    
    
    
    
    
    if (!m_VAOReady)
    {
        glGenVertexArrays(1, &m_VAO);
        glGenBuffers(1, &m_VBO);
        glGenBuffers(1, &m_IBO);

        glBindVertexArray(m_VAO);
        glBindBuffer(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, m_VBO);
        glBindBuffer(GL_ELEMENT_ARRAY_BUFFER, m_IBO);

        
        glEnableVertexAttribArray(0);
        glVertexAttribPointer(0, 3, GL_FLOAT, GL_FALSE, sizeof(Vertex),
                              (void*)offsetof(Vertex, Position));
        
        glEnableVertexAttribArray(1);
        glVertexAttribPointer(1, 3, GL_FLOAT, GL_FALSE, sizeof(Vertex),
                              (void*)offsetof(Vertex, Normal));
        
        glEnableVertexAttribArray(2);
        glVertexAttribPointer(2, 2, GL_FLOAT, GL_FALSE, sizeof(Vertex),
                              (void*)offsetof(Vertex, UV));
        
        glEnableVertexAttribArray(3);
        glVertexAttribIPointer(3, 4, GL_UNSIGNED_BYTE, sizeof(Vertex),
                               (void*)offsetof(Vertex, BoneIndices));
        
        glEnableVertexAttribArray(4);
        glVertexAttribPointer(4, 4, GL_UNSIGNED_BYTE, GL_TRUE, sizeof(Vertex),
                              (void*)offsetof(Vertex, BoneWeights));

        m_VAOReady = true;
    }

    
    glBindVertexArray(m_VAO);

    glBindBuffer(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, m_VBO);
    glBufferData(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER,
                 (GLsizeiptr)(m_CPUVertices.size() * sizeof(Vertex)),
                 m_CPUVertices.data(), GL_STATIC_DRAW);

    glBindBuffer(GL_ELEMENT_ARRAY_BUFFER, m_IBO);
    glBufferData(GL_ELEMENT_ARRAY_BUFFER,
                 (GLsizeiptr)(m_CPUIndices.size() * sizeof(uint32_t)),
                 m_CPUIndices.data(), GL_STATIC_DRAW);

    
    glBindBuffer(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, 0);
    glBindVertexArray(0);

    
    if (m_MeshSSBO == 0)
        glGenBuffers(1, &m_MeshSSBO);
    glBindBuffer(GL_SHADER_STORAGE_BUFFER, m_MeshSSBO);
    glBufferData(GL_SHADER_STORAGE_BUFFER,
                 (GLsizeiptr)(m_Meshes.size() * sizeof(MeshGPU)),
                 m_Meshes.data(), GL_STATIC_DRAW);
    glBindBuffer(GL_SHADER_STORAGE_BUFFER, 0);

    m_Dirty = false;
}

void MeshLibrary::Clear()
{
    m_CPUVertices.clear();
    m_CPUIndices.clear();
    m_Meshes.clear();
    m_Names.clear();
    m_ByName.clear();
    m_Dirty = true;

    if (m_VAO)      { glDeleteVertexArrays(1, &m_VAO);      m_VAO = 0; }
    if (m_VBO)      { glDeleteBuffers(1, &m_VBO);           m_VBO = 0; }
    if (m_IBO)      { glDeleteBuffers(1, &m_IBO);           m_IBO = 0; }
    if (m_MeshSSBO) { glDeleteBuffers(1, &m_MeshSSBO);      m_MeshSSBO = 0; }
    m_VAOReady = false;
}

}

================================================================================
FILE: Renderer/MeshLibrary.h
================================================================================
#pragma once

#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <vector>

#include "Renderer/Vertex.h"

namespace Binax {

using MeshHandle = uint32_t;
constexpr MeshHandle kInvalidMesh = 0xFFFFFFFFu;

struct MeshGPU
{
    uint32_t  BaseVertex  = 0;
    uint32_t  VertexCount = 0;
    uint32_t  BaseIndex   = 0;
    uint32_t  IndexCount  = 0;
    glm::vec3 AABBMin     = glm::vec3(0.0f);
    float     _pad0       = 0.0f;
    glm::vec3 AABBMax     = glm::vec3(0.0f);
    float     _pad1       = 0.0f;
};
static_assert(sizeof(MeshGPU) == 48, "MeshGPU layout");

class MeshLibrary
{
public:
    static MeshLibrary& Get();

    MeshHandle Add(const std::string& name,
                   const std::vector<Vertex>& vertices,
                   const std::vector<uint32_t>& indices);
    MeshHandle Find(const std::string& name) const;

    bool IsValid(MeshHandle h) const { return h < m_Meshes.size(); }

    const MeshGPU&     Get(MeshHandle h) const     { return m_Meshes[h]; }
    const std::string& GetName(MeshHandle h) const { return m_Names[h]; }
    uint32_t           GetMeshCount() const        { return (uint32_t)m_Meshes.size(); }

    void BindVAO() const;
    void Flush();
    void Clear();

    uint32_t GetMeshSSBO() const { return m_MeshSSBO; }

private:
    MeshLibrary() = default;

    std::vector<Vertex>         m_CPUVertices;
    std::vector<uint32_t>       m_CPUIndices;
    std::vector<MeshGPU>        m_Meshes;
    std::vector<std::string>    m_Names;
    std::unordered_map<std::string, MeshHandle> m_ByName;

    uint32_t m_VAO = 0;
    uint32_t m_VBO = 0;
    uint32_t m_IBO = 0;
    uint32_t m_MeshSSBO = 0;
    bool     m_Dirty = false;
    bool     m_VAOReady = false;
};

}

================================================================================
FILE: Renderer/Shader.cpp
================================================================================

#include "Shader.h"
#include "../Utils/Log.h"
#include "../Utils/Paths.h"
#include <glad/glad.h>
#include <fstream>
#include <sstream>
#include <filesystem>
#include <stdexcept>

namespace Binax {

namespace {

std::string ReadTextFile(const std::string& path)
{
    std::ifstream file(Paths::FromUtf8(path), std::ios::binary);
    if (!file.is_open())
        throw std::runtime_error("Failed to open shader file: " + path);

    std::stringstream ss;
    ss << file.rdbuf();

    if (ss.str().empty())
        throw std::runtime_error("Shader file is empty: " + path);

    return ss.str();
}

} 



Shader::Shader(const std::string& vertexSrc, const std::string& fragmentSrc,
               Stage stage)
    : m_Stage(stage)
{
    m_RendererID = BuildProgram(vertexSrc, fragmentSrc, m_Stage);
}

std::unique_ptr<Shader> Shader::FromFiles(const std::string& vertexPath,
                                          const std::string& fragmentPath,
                                          Stage stage)
{
    std::string vsrc = ReadTextFile(vertexPath);
    std::string fsrc = (stage == Stage::Compute) ? std::string{} : ReadTextFile(fragmentPath);

    auto shader = std::unique_ptr<Shader>(new Shader(vsrc, fsrc, stage));
    shader->m_VertexPath   = vertexPath;
    shader->m_FragmentPath = fragmentPath;

    if (stage == Stage::Compute)
        BINAX_LOG_INFO("Compute shader loaded: {0}", vertexPath);
    else
        BINAX_LOG_INFO("Shader loaded: {0} + {1}", vertexPath, fragmentPath);

    return shader;
}

void Shader::Reload()
{
    if (m_VertexPath.empty() || m_FragmentPath.empty())
    {
        BINAX_LOG_WARN("Shader::Reload() called on source-only shader (no-op)");
        return;
    }

    try
    {
        const std::string vsrc = ReadTextFile(m_VertexPath);
        const std::string fsrc = (m_Stage == Stage::Compute)
                                     ? std::string{}
                                     : ReadTextFile(m_FragmentPath);

        
        
        const uint32_t newID = BuildProgram(vsrc, fsrc, m_Stage);

        if (m_RendererID != 0)
            glDeleteProgram(m_RendererID);
        m_RendererID = newID;
        m_UniformLocationCache.clear();

        BINAX_LOG_INFO("Shader reloaded: {0} + {1}", m_VertexPath, m_FragmentPath);
    }
    catch (const std::exception& e)
    {
        BINAX_LOG_ERROR("Shader reload failed, keeping old program: {0}", e.what());
    }
}

Shader::~Shader()
{
    if (m_RendererID != 0)
        glDeleteProgram(m_RendererID);
}



void Shader::Bind() const   { glUseProgram(m_RendererID); }
void Shader::Unbind() const { glUseProgram(0); }

int Shader::GetUniformLocation(const std::string& name) const
{
    auto it = m_UniformLocationCache.find(name);
    if (it != m_UniformLocationCache.end())
        return it->second;

    int location = glGetUniformLocation(m_RendererID, name.c_str());
    m_UniformLocationCache[name] = location;
    return location;
}

void Shader::SetInt  (const std::string& n, int v)               { glUniform1i(GetUniformLocation(n), v); }
void Shader::SetUInt (const std::string& n, uint32_t v)          { glUniform1ui(GetUniformLocation(n), v); }
void Shader::SetFloat(const std::string& n, float v)             { glUniform1f(GetUniformLocation(n), v); }
void Shader::SetVec2 (const std::string& n, const glm::vec2& v)  { glUniform2fv(GetUniformLocation(n), 1, &v[0]); }
void Shader::SetVec3 (const std::string& n, const glm::vec3& v)  { glUniform3fv(GetUniformLocation(n), 1, &v[0]); }
void Shader::SetVec4 (const std::string& n, const glm::vec4& v)  { glUniform4fv(GetUniformLocation(n), 1, &v[0]); }
void Shader::SetMat4 (const std::string& n, const glm::mat4& v)  { glUniformMatrix4fv(GetUniformLocation(n), 1, GL_FALSE, &v[0][0]); }



uint32_t Shader::BuildProgram(const std::string& vertexSrc,
                              const std::string& fragmentSrc,
                              Stage stage)
{
    if (stage == Stage::Compute)
    {
        const uint32_t cs = CompileShader(GL_COMPUTE_SHADER, vertexSrc);
        const uint32_t program = glCreateProgram();
        glAttachShader(program, cs);
        glLinkProgram(program);

        int success = 0;
        glGetProgramiv(program, GL_LINK_STATUS, &success);
        if (!success)
        {
            char infoLog[1024];
            glGetProgramInfoLog(program, 1024, nullptr, infoLog);
            glDeleteProgram(program);
            glDeleteShader(cs);
            throw std::runtime_error(std::string("Compute program linking failed:\n") + infoLog);
        }

        glDeleteShader(cs);
        return program;
    }

    const uint32_t vs = CompileShader(GL_VERTEX_SHADER, vertexSrc);

    
    
    
    uint32_t fs = 0;
    try
    {
        fs = CompileShader(GL_FRAGMENT_SHADER, fragmentSrc);
    }
    catch (...)
    {
        glDeleteShader(vs);
        throw;
    }

    const uint32_t program = glCreateProgram();
    glAttachShader(program, vs);
    glAttachShader(program, fs);
    glLinkProgram(program);

    int success = 0;
    glGetProgramiv(program, GL_LINK_STATUS, &success);
    if (!success)
    {
        char infoLog[1024];
        glGetProgramInfoLog(program, 1024, nullptr, infoLog);
        glDeleteProgram(program);
        glDeleteShader(vs);
        glDeleteShader(fs);
        throw std::runtime_error(std::string("Shader program linking failed:\n") + infoLog);
    }

    glDeleteShader(vs);
    glDeleteShader(fs);
    return program;
}

uint32_t Shader::CompileShader(uint32_t type, const std::string& source)
{
    uint32_t shader = glCreateShader(type);
    const char* src = source.c_str();
    glShaderSource(shader, 1, &src, nullptr);
    glCompileShader(shader);

    int success = 0;
    glGetShaderiv(shader, GL_COMPILE_STATUS, &success);
    if (!success)
    {
        char infoLog[1024];
        glGetShaderInfoLog(shader, 1024, nullptr, infoLog);
        const char* typeName = (type == GL_VERTEX_SHADER)   ? "Vertex"
                             : (type == GL_COMPUTE_SHADER)  ? "Compute"
                                                            : "Fragment";
        glDeleteShader(shader);
        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(typeName) + " shader compilation failed:\n" + infoLog);
    }
    return shader;
}

}

================================================================================

#pragma once

#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <memory>

namespace Binax {

class Shader
{
public:
    
    enum class Stage
    {
        Graphics,
        Compute
    };

    
    Shader(const std::string& vertexSrc, const std::string& fragmentSrc,
           Stage stage = Stage::Graphics);

    
    
    static std::unique_ptr<Shader> FromFiles(const std::string& vertexPath,
                                             const std::string& fragmentPath,
                                             Stage stage = Stage::Graphics);

    ~Shader();

    Shader(const Shader&) = delete;
    Shader& operator=(const Shader&) = delete;

    void Bind() const;
    void Unbind() const;

    void SetInt  (const std::string& name, int value);
    
    
    void SetUInt (const std::string& name, uint32_t value);
    void SetFloat(const std::string& name, float value);
    void SetVec2 (const std::string& name, const glm::vec2& value);
    void SetVec3 (const std::string& name, const glm::vec3& value);
    void SetVec4 (const std::string& name, const glm::vec4& value);
    void SetMat4 (const std::string& name, const glm::mat4& value);

    
    void Reload();

    Stage GetStage() const { return m_Stage; }

private:
    uint32_t m_RendererID = 0;
    mutable std::unordered_map<std::string, int> m_UniformLocationCache;

    Stage       m_Stage = Stage::Graphics;
    std::string m_VertexPath;
    std::string m_FragmentPath;

    int GetUniformLocation(const std::string& name) const;

    static uint32_t BuildProgram(const std::string& vertexSrc,
                                 const std::string& fragmentSrc,
                                 Stage stage);

    static uint32_t CompileShader(uint32_t type, const std::string& source);
};

}

================================================================================
FILE: Renderer/Texture.cpp
================================================================================
#include "Texture.h"
#include "Utils/Log.h"
#include "Utils/FileIO.h"

#include <glad/glad.h>

#define STB_IMAGE_IMPLEMENTATION
#include <stb/stb_image.h>

namespace Binax {

Texture::~Texture()
{
    if (m_ID)
        glDeleteTextures(1, &m_ID);
}

Texture::Texture(Texture&& other) noexcept
    : m_ID(other.m_ID)
    , m_Width(other.m_Width)
    , m_Height(other.m_Height)
    , m_Channels(other.m_Channels)
    , m_Path(std::move(other.m_Path))
{
    other.m_ID = 0;
}

Texture& Texture::operator=(Texture&& other) noexcept
{
    if (this != &other)
    {
        if (m_ID) glDeleteTextures(1, &m_ID);
        m_ID       = other.m_ID;
        m_Width    = other.m_Width;
        m_Height   = other.m_Height;
        m_Channels = other.m_Channels;
        m_Path     = std::move(other.m_Path);
        other.m_ID = 0;
    }
    return *this;
}

std::unique_ptr<Texture> Texture::LoadFromFile(const std::string& path, bool srgb)
{
    stbi_set_flip_vertically_on_load(1);

    
    
    
    std::FILE* file = OpenFileUtf8(path);
    if (!file)
    {
        BINAX_LOG_ERROR("Texture: cannot open '{}'", path);
        return nullptr;
    }

    int w = 0, h = 0, ch = 0;
    unsigned char* data = stbi_load_from_file(file, &w, &h, &ch, 0);
    std::fclose(file);

    if (!data)
    {
        BINAX_LOG_ERROR("Texture load failed '{}': {}", path, stbi_failure_reason());
        return nullptr;
    }

    GLenum internalFmt = GL_RGB8;
    GLenum fmt         = GL_RGB;

    
    
    
    
    if (ch == 1)      { internalFmt = GL_R8;  fmt = GL_RED;  }
    else if (ch == 2) { internalFmt = GL_RG8; fmt = GL_RG;   }
    else if (ch == 3) { internalFmt = srgb ? GL_SRGB8        : GL_RGB8;  fmt = GL_RGB;  }
    else if (ch == 4) { internalFmt = srgb ? GL_SRGB8_ALPHA8 : GL_RGBA8; fmt = GL_RGBA; }

    auto tex = std::unique_ptr<Texture>(new Texture());
    glGenTextures(1, &tex->m_ID);
    glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D, tex->m_ID);
    glTexImage2D(GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0, internalFmt, w, h, 0, fmt, GL_UNSIGNED_BYTE, data);
    glGenerateMipmap(GL_TEXTURE_2D);

    glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_S, GL_REPEAT);
    glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_T, GL_REPEAT);
    glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, GL_LINEAR_MIPMAP_LINEAR);
    glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, GL_LINEAR);

#ifdef GL_TEXTURE_MAX_ANISOTROPY_EXT
    if (GLAD_GL_EXT_texture_filter_anisotropic)
    {
        GLfloat maxAniso = 1.0f;
        glGetFloatv(GL_MAX_TEXTURE_MAX_ANISOTROPY_EXT, &maxAniso);
        if (maxAniso > 1.0f)
            glTexParameterf(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_MAX_ANISOTROPY_EXT, maxAniso);
    }
#endif

    stbi_image_free(data);
    glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0);

    tex->m_Width    = w;
    tex->m_Height   = h;
    tex->m_Channels = ch;
    tex->m_Path     = path;
    return tex;
}

std::unique_ptr<Texture> Texture::CreateSolid(uint8_t r, uint8_t g, uint8_t b, uint8_t a)
{
    auto tex = std::unique_ptr<Texture>(new Texture());
    uint8_t px[4] = { r, g, b, a };

    glGenTextures(1, &tex->m_ID);
    glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D, tex->m_ID);
    glTexImage2D(GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0, GL_RGBA8, 1, 1, 0, GL_RGBA, GL_UNSIGNED_BYTE, px);
    glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_S, GL_REPEAT);
    glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_T, GL_REPEAT);
    glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, GL_NEAREST);
    glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, GL_NEAREST);
    glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0);

    tex->m_Width    = 1;
    tex->m_Height   = 1;
    tex->m_Channels = 4;
    return tex;
}

void Texture::Bind(uint32_t slot) const
{
    glActiveTexture(GL_TEXTURE0 + slot);
    glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D, m_ID);
}

void Texture::Unbind(uint32_t slot) const
{
    glActiveTexture(GL_TEXTURE0 + slot);
    glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0);
}

}

================================================================================
FILE: Serialization/JsonHelpers.h
================================================================================
#pragma once

#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <nlohmann/json.hpp>
#include <string>



namespace Binax::Serialization::Json
{

inline float Number(const nlohmann::json& j, const char* key, float def)
{
    const auto it = j.find(key);
    return (it != j.end() && it->is_number()) ? it->get<float>() : def;
}

inline int Int(const nlohmann::json& j, const char* key, int def)
{
    const auto it = j.find(key);
    return (it != j.end() && it->is_number_integer()) ? it->get<int>() : def;
}

inline bool Bool(const nlohmann::json& j, const char* key, bool def)
{
    const auto it = j.find(key);
    return (it != j.end() && it->is_boolean()) ? it->get<bool>() : def;
}

inline std::string String(const nlohmann::json& j, const char* key)
{
    const auto it = j.find(key);
    return (it != j.end() && it->is_string()) ? it->get<std::string>() : std::string{};
}

inline glm::vec2 Vec2(const nlohmann::json& j, const char* key, glm::vec2 def)
{
    const auto it = j.find(key);
    if (it == j.end() || !it->is_array() || it->size() < 2)
        return def;
    for (int i = 0; i < 2; ++i)
        if (!(*it)[i].is_number())
            return def;
    return glm::vec2((*it)[0].get<float>(), (*it)[1].get<float>());
}

inline glm::vec3 Vec3(const nlohmann::json& j, const char* key, glm::vec3 def)
{
    const auto it = j.find(key);
    if (it == j.end() || !it->is_array() || it->size() < 3)
        return def;
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i)
        if (!(*it)[i].is_number())
            return def;
    return glm::vec3((*it)[0].get<float>(), (*it)[1].get<float>(), (*it)[2].get<float>());
}

inline void SetVec3(nlohmann::json& j, const char* key, const glm::vec3& v)
{
    j[key] = { v.x, v.y, v.z };
}

}

================================================================================
FILE: Serialization/MaterialSerializer.cpp
================================================================================
#include "MaterialSerializer.h"
#include "Serialization/JsonHelpers.h"
#include "Utils/AssetPaths.h"
#include "Utils/Log.h"
#include "Utils/Paths.h"

#include <nlohmann/json.hpp>
#include <filesystem>
#include <fstream>

namespace Binax
{

namespace fs = std::filesystem;
using namespace Serialization::Json;

std::string MaterialSerializer::SanitizeFileName(const std::string& name)
{
    std::string out;
    out.reserve(name.size());
    for (char c : name)
    {
        switch (c)
        {
        case '\\': case '/': case ':': case '*': case '?':
        case '"':  case '<': case '>': case '|':
            out.push_back('_');
            break;
        default:
            out.push_back(c);
            break;
        }
    }
    if (out.empty())
        out = "material";
    return out;
}

bool MaterialSerializer::Save(const MaterialLibrary& lib, MaterialHandle handle,
                              const std::string& filepath)
{
    if (!lib.IsValid(handle))
        return false;

    const MaterialGPU& gpu = lib.Get(handle);
    const MaterialEditData& ed = lib.GetEdit(handle);

    nlohmann::json j;
    j["version"] = kVersion;
    j["name"]    = ed.Name;

    j["albedo"]    = { gpu.AlbedoRoughness.x, gpu.AlbedoRoughness.y, gpu.AlbedoRoughness.z };
    j["roughness"] = gpu.AlbedoRoughness.w;

    j["metallic"] = gpu.MetallicAOEms.x;
    j["ao"]       = gpu.MetallicAOEms.y;
    j["emissive"] = gpu.MetallicAOEms.z;
    j["tiling"]   = gpu.MetallicAOEms.w;

    j["skyReflectionsEnabled"] = (gpu.SkyReflParams.x > 0.5f);
    j["skyReflectionStrength"] = gpu.SkyReflParams.y;

    j["metallicStrength"]  = gpu.StrengthA.x;
    j["roughnessStrength"] = gpu.StrengthA.y;
    j["aoStrength"]        = gpu.StrengthA.z;
    j["emissiveStrength"]  = gpu.StrengthA.w;

    j["albedoStrength"] = gpu.StrengthB.x;
    j["normalStrength"] = gpu.StrengthB.y;

    j["albedoMap"]    = ed.AlbedoMapPath;
    j["normalMap"]    = ed.NormalMapPath;
    j["metallicMap"]  = ed.MetallicMapPath;
    j["roughnessMap"] = ed.RoughnessMapPath;
    j["aoMap"]        = ed.AOMapPath;
    j["emissiveMap"]  = ed.EmissiveMapPath;

    const fs::path path = Paths::FromUtf8(filepath);
    std::error_code ec;
    if (path.has_parent_path())
        fs::create_directories(path.parent_path(), ec);

    std::ofstream file(path);
    if (!file.is_open())
    {
        BINAX_LOG_ERROR("Material: cannot write '{}'", filepath);
        return false;
    }

    file << j.dump(4);
    file.flush();
    const bool ok = file.good();
    return ok;
}

bool MaterialSerializer::SaveAll(const MaterialLibrary& lib, const std::string& directory)
{
    std::error_code ec;
    const fs::path dir = Paths::FromUtf8(directory);
    fs::create_directories(dir, ec);

    bool allOk = true;
    uint32_t saved = 0;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < lib.GetMaterialCount(); ++i)
    {
        const MaterialHandle h = i;
        const std::string& name = lib.GetName(h);
        if (name.empty() || name == AssetFiles::kDefaultMaterialName)
            continue;

        const fs::path filePath =
            dir / (SanitizeFileName(name) + AssetFiles::kMaterialExt);

        if (Save(lib, h, Paths::ToUtf8(filePath)))
            ++saved;
        else
            allOk = false;
    }

    BINAX_LOG_INFO("Materials saved: {} -> {}", saved, directory);
    return allOk;
}

bool MaterialSerializer::Load(MaterialLibrary& lib, const std::string& filepath)
{
    std::ifstream file(Paths::FromUtf8(filepath));
    if (!file.is_open())
    {
        BINAX_LOG_ERROR("Material: cannot open '{}'", filepath);
        return false;
    }

    nlohmann::json j;
    try
    {
        file >> j;
    }
    catch (const std::exception& e)
    {
        BINAX_LOG_ERROR("Material '{}': parse error: {}", filepath, e.what());
        return false;
    }

    if (!j.is_object() || Int(j, "version", 0) != kVersion)
    {
        BINAX_LOG_ERROR("Material '{}': unsupported version", filepath);
        return false;
    }

    const std::string name = String(j, "name");
    if (name.empty())
    {
        BINAX_LOG_WARN("Material '{}': empty name — skipped", filepath);
        return false;
    }

    const glm::vec3 albedo = Vec3(j, "albedo", glm::vec3(1.0f));

    MaterialGPU gpu{};
    gpu.AlbedoRoughness = glm::vec4(albedo, Number(j, "roughness", 0.5f));
    gpu.MetallicAOEms   = glm::vec4(Number(j, "metallic", 0.0f),
                                    Number(j, "ao",       1.0f),
                                    Number(j, "emissive", 0.0f),
                                    Number(j, "tiling",   1.0f));
    gpu.SkyReflParams   = glm::vec4(Bool(j, "skyReflectionsEnabled", true) ? 1.0f : 0.0f,
                                    Number(j, "skyReflectionStrength", 1.0f),
                                    0.0f, 0.0f);
    gpu.StrengthA       = glm::vec4(Number(j, "metallicStrength",  1.0f),
                                    Number(j, "roughnessStrength", 1.0f),
                                    Number(j, "aoStrength",        1.0f),
                                    Number(j, "emissiveStrength",  1.0f));
    gpu.StrengthB       = glm::vec4(Number(j, "albedoStrength", 1.0f),
                                    Number(j, "normalStrength", 1.0f),
                                    0.0f, 0.0f);

    const MaterialHandle h = lib.Add(name, gpu);

    lib.SetAlbedoMap   (h, String(j, "albedoMap"));
    lib.SetNormalMap   (h, String(j, "normalMap"));
    lib.SetMetallicMap (h, String(j, "metallicMap"));
    lib.SetRoughnessMap(h, String(j, "roughnessMap"));
    lib.SetAOMap       (h, String(j, "aoMap"));
    lib.SetEmissiveMap (h, String(j, "emissiveMap"));

    return true;
}

void MaterialSerializer::LoadAll(MaterialLibrary& lib, const std::string& directory)
{
    lib.Clear();

    std::error_code ec;
    const fs::path dir = Paths::FromUtf8(directory);
    if (fs::exists(dir, ec) && !ec && fs::is_directory(dir, ec) && !ec)
    {
        try
        {
            for (const auto& entry : fs::directory_iterator(dir, ec))
            {
                if (ec)
                    break;
                std::error_code fileEc;
                if (!entry.is_regular_file(fileEc) || fileEc)
                    continue;
                if (entry.path().extension() != AssetFiles::kMaterialExt)
                    continue;
                Load(lib, Paths::ToUtf8(entry.path()));
            }
        }
        catch (const std::filesystem::filesystem_error& e)
        {
            BINAX_LOG_WARN("Materials: cannot list '{}': {}", directory, e.what());
        }
    }

    lib.GetOrCreateDefault();
    lib.Flush();

    BINAX_LOG_INFO("Materials loaded from {0}: {1}", directory,
                   lib.GetMaterialCount());
}

}

================================================================================
FILE: Serialization/PostProcessSerializer.cpp
================================================================================
#include "PostProcessSerializer.h"
#include "Serialization/JsonHelpers.h"
#include "ECS/Components/PostProcessComponent.h"
#include "ECS/Components/NameComponent.h"
#include "ECS/Components/SystemEntityTag.h"
#include "ECS/Components/Transform.h"
#include "Utils/Log.h"
#include "Utils/Paths.h"

#include <nlohmann/json.hpp>
#include <filesystem>
#include <fstream>

namespace Binax
{

using namespace Serialization::Json;

namespace
{

PostProcessComponent* FindSettings(entt::registry& registry)
{
    auto view = registry.view<PostProcessComponent>();
    for (auto entity : view)
        return &view.get<PostProcessComponent>(entity);
    return nullptr;
}



PostProcessComponent* EnsureSettings(entt::registry& registry)
{
    if (auto* existing = FindSettings(registry))
        return existing;

    auto entity = registry.create();
    registry.emplace<NameComponent>(entity, "PostProcessingCfg");
    registry.emplace<Transform>(entity);
    registry.emplace<SystemEntityTag>(entity);
    return &registry.emplace<PostProcessComponent>(entity);
}

} 

bool PostProcessSerializer::Save(entt::registry& registry, const std::string& filepath)
{
    const PostProcessComponent* c = FindSettings(registry);
    if (!c)
    {
        BINAX_LOG_WARN("PostProcessing: no config component to save");
        return false;
    }

    nlohmann::json j;
    j["version"] = kVersion;

    j["enabled"]  = c->Enabled;
    j["exposure"] = c->Exposure;
    j["tonemap"]  = static_cast<int>(c->Tonemap);

    
    j["contrast"]    = c->Contrast;
    j["saturation"]  = c->Saturation;
    j["temperature"] = c->Temperature;
    j["tint"]        = c->Tint;
    SetVec3(j, "lift",  c->Lift);
    SetVec3(j, "gamma", c->Gamma);
    SetVec3(j, "gain",  c->Gain);

    
    j["bloomEnabled"]   = c->BloomEnabled;
    j["bloomThreshold"] = c->BloomThreshold;
    j["bloomIntensity"] = c->BloomIntensity;

    
    j["motionBlurEnabled"]  = c->MotionBlurEnabled;
    j["motionBlurStrength"] = c->MotionBlurStrength;
    j["motionBlurSamples"]  = c->MotionBlurSamples;

    
    j["lensDistortionEnabled"] = c->LensDistortionEnabled;
    j["lensDistortion"]        = c->LensDistortion;
    j["chromaticAberrationEnabled"] = c->ChromaticAberrationEnabled;
    j["chromaticAberration"]        = c->ChromaticAberration;

    
    j["vignetteEnabled"]    = c->VignetteEnabled;
    j["vignetteIntensity"]  = c->VignetteIntensity;
    j["vignetteSmoothness"] = c->VignetteSmoothness;
    j["filmGrainEnabled"]   = c->FilmGrainEnabled;
    j["filmGrainIntensity"] = c->FilmGrainIntensity;

    
    j["fogEnabled"] = c->FogEnabled;
    j["fogMode"]    = static_cast<int>(c->Fog);
    SetVec3(j, "fogColor", c->FogColor);
    j["fogDensity"] = c->FogDensity;
    j["fogStart"]   = c->FogStart;
    j["fogEnd"]     = c->FogEnd;

    const std::filesystem::path path = Paths::FromUtf8(filepath);
    std::error_code ec;
    if (path.has_parent_path())
        std::filesystem::create_directories(path.parent_path(), ec);

    std::ofstream file(path);
    if (!file.is_open())
    {
        BINAX_LOG_ERROR("PostProcessing: cannot write '{}'", filepath);
        return false;
    }

    file << j.dump(4);
    file.flush();
    const bool ok = file.good();
    if (ok)
        BINAX_LOG_INFO("Post-processing settings saved to {}", filepath);
    return ok;
}

bool PostProcessSerializer::Load(entt::registry& registry, const std::string& filepath)
{
    std::ifstream file(Paths::FromUtf8(filepath));
    if (!file.is_open())
    {
        BINAX_LOG_ERROR("PostProcessing: cannot open '{}'", filepath);
        return false;
    }

    nlohmann::json j;
    try
    {
        file >> j;
    }
    catch (const std::exception& e)
    {
        BINAX_LOG_ERROR("PostProcessing '{}': parse error: {}", filepath, e.what());
        return false;
    }

    if (!j.is_object() || Int(j, "version", 0) != kVersion)
    {
        BINAX_LOG_ERROR("PostProcessing '{}': unsupported version", filepath);
        return false;
    }

    PostProcessComponent* c = EnsureSettings(registry);

    c->Enabled  = Bool(j, "enabled", true);
    c->Exposure = Number(j, "exposure", 1.0f);

    const int tonemap = Int(j, "tonemap", 0);
    if (tonemap >= static_cast<int>(TonemapMode::ACES) &&
        tonemap <= static_cast<int>(TonemapMode::Linear))
        c->Tonemap = static_cast<TonemapMode>(tonemap);

    c->Contrast    = Number(j, "contrast", 1.0f);
    c->Saturation  = Number(j, "saturation", 1.0f);
    c->Temperature = Number(j, "temperature", 0.0f);
    c->Tint        = Number(j, "tint", 0.0f);
    c->Lift  = Vec3(j, "lift",  glm::vec3(0.0f));
    c->Gamma = Vec3(j, "gamma", glm::vec3(1.0f));
    c->Gain  = Vec3(j, "gain",  glm::vec3(1.0f));

    c->BloomEnabled   = Bool(j, "bloomEnabled", true);
    c->BloomThreshold = Number(j, "bloomThreshold", 1.0f);
    c->BloomIntensity = Number(j, "bloomIntensity", 0.15f);

    c->MotionBlurEnabled  = Bool(j, "motionBlurEnabled", true);
    c->MotionBlurStrength = Number(j, "motionBlurStrength", 0.5f);
    c->MotionBlurSamples  = Int(j, "motionBlurSamples", 8);

    c->LensDistortionEnabled = Bool(j, "lensDistortionEnabled", false);
    c->LensDistortion        = Number(j, "lensDistortion", 0.0f);
    c->ChromaticAberrationEnabled = Bool(j, "chromaticAberrationEnabled", false);
    c->ChromaticAberration        = Number(j, "chromaticAberration", 0.0f);

    c->VignetteEnabled    = Bool(j, "vignetteEnabled", true);
    c->VignetteIntensity  = Number(j, "vignetteIntensity", 0.35f);
    c->VignetteSmoothness = Number(j, "vignetteSmoothness", 0.5f);
    c->FilmGrainEnabled   = Bool(j, "filmGrainEnabled", false);
    c->FilmGrainIntensity = Number(j, "filmGrainIntensity", 0.08f);

    c->FogEnabled = Bool(j, "fogEnabled", false);
    const int fogMode = Int(j, "fogMode", 1);
    if (fogMode >= static_cast<int>(FogMode::Linear) &&
        fogMode <= static_cast<int>(FogMode::ExponentialSquared))
        c->Fog = static_cast<FogMode>(fogMode);
    c->FogColor   = Vec3(j, "fogColor", glm::vec3(0.62f, 0.70f, 0.80f));
    c->FogDensity = Number(j, "fogDensity", 0.04f);
    c->FogStart   = Number(j, "fogStart", 5.0f);
    c->FogEnd     = Number(j, "fogEnd", 60.0f);

    BINAX_LOG_INFO("Post-processing settings loaded from {}", filepath);
    return true;
}

}

================================================================================
FILE: Serialization/PropertySerializer.cpp
================================================================================
#include "Serialization/PropertySerializer.h"
#include "Reflect/Reflection.h"
#include "Reflect/ComponentRegistry.h"
#include "Utils/Log.h"

#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <glm/gtc/quaternion.hpp>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cstring>

namespace Binax::Serialization {

using namespace Reflect;

namespace {



size_t EnumSize(const PropertyInfo& p)
{
    return std::min<size_t>(p.Size ? p.Size : sizeof(int), sizeof(int));
}

int ReadEnum(const void* obj, const PropertyInfo& p)
{
    int value = 0;
    std::memcpy(&value, p.GetRaw(obj), EnumSize(p));
    return value;
}

void WriteEnum(void* obj, const PropertyInfo& p, int value)
{
    std::memcpy(p.GetRaw(obj), &value, EnumSize(p));
}




bool ReadFloats(const nlohmann::json& j, float* out, std::size_t count,
                const char* propName)
{
    if (!j.is_array() || j.size() < count)
    {
        BINAX_LOG_WARN("Property '{}': expected array of {} numbers", propName, count);
        return false;
    }

    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i)
        out[i] = j[i].get<float>();

    return true;
}

nlohmann::json SerializeProperty(const void* obj, const PropertyInfo& p)
{
    using PK = PropertyKind;
    switch (p.Kind)
    {
    case PK::Bool:   return *p.GetPtr<bool>(obj);
    case PK::Int:    return *p.GetPtr<int>(obj);
    case PK::UInt:   return *p.GetPtr<uint32_t>(obj);
    case PK::Float:  return *p.GetPtr<float>(obj);
    case PK::Double: return *p.GetPtr<double>(obj);
    case PK::String: return *p.GetPtr<std::string>(obj);
    case PK::Asset:  return *p.GetPtr<std::string>(obj);
    case PK::Enum:   return ReadEnum(obj, p);
    case PK::Vec2:   { auto* v = p.GetPtr<glm::vec2>(obj); return nlohmann::json{ v->x, v->y }; }
    case PK::Vec3:
    case PK::Color3: { auto* v = p.GetPtr<glm::vec3>(obj); return nlohmann::json{ v->x, v->y, v->z }; }
    case PK::Vec4:
    case PK::Color4: { auto* v = p.GetPtr<glm::vec4>(obj); return nlohmann::json{ v->x, v->y, v->z, v->w }; }
    case PK::Quat:   { auto* q = p.GetPtr<glm::quat>(obj); return nlohmann::json{ q->w, q->x, q->y, q->z }; }
    default:         return nullptr;
    }
}

void DeserializeProperty(void* obj, const PropertyInfo& p, const nlohmann::json& j)
{
    using PK = PropertyKind;
    try {
        switch (p.Kind)
        {
        case PK::Bool:   *p.GetPtr<bool>(obj)        = j.get<bool>();        break;
        case PK::Int:    *p.GetPtr<int>(obj)         = j.get<int>();         break;
        case PK::UInt:   *p.GetPtr<uint32_t>(obj)    = j.get<uint32_t>();    break;
        case PK::Float:  *p.GetPtr<float>(obj)       = j.get<float>();       break;
        case PK::Double: *p.GetPtr<double>(obj)      = j.get<double>();      break;
        case PK::String: *p.GetPtr<std::string>(obj) = j.get<std::string>(); break;
        case PK::Asset:  *p.GetPtr<std::string>(obj) = j.get<std::string>(); break;
        case PK::Enum:   WriteEnum(obj, p, j.get<int>()); break;
        case PK::Vec2:   { auto* v = p.GetPtr<glm::vec2>(obj); ReadFloats(j, &v->x, 2, p.Name.c_str()); break; }
        case PK::Vec3:
        case PK::Color3: { auto* v = p.GetPtr<glm::vec3>(obj); ReadFloats(j, &v->x, 3, p.Name.c_str()); break; }
        case PK::Vec4:
        case PK::Color4: { auto* v = p.GetPtr<glm::vec4>(obj); ReadFloats(j, &v->x, 4, p.Name.c_str()); break; }
        case PK::Quat:   { auto* q = p.GetPtr<glm::quat>(obj);
                           
                           float wxyz[4]{};
                           if (ReadFloats(j, wxyz, 4, p.Name.c_str()))
                           {
                               q->w = wxyz[0]; q->x = wxyz[1];
                               q->y = wxyz[2]; q->z = wxyz[3];
                           }
                           break; }
        default: break;
        }
    } catch (const std::exception& e) {
        BINAX_LOG_WARN("Failed to read property '{}': {}", p.Name, e.what());
    }
}

} 

nlohmann::json PropertySerializer::SerializeEntity(entt::registry& reg, entt::entity e)
{
    nlohmann::json out;
    auto& cr = ComponentRegistry::Get();

    for (const auto& entryPtr : cr.All())
    {
        const auto& entry = *entryPtr;
        if (entry.Transient)         continue;
        if (!entry.Has(reg, e))      continue;

        const ClassInfo* info = TypeRegistry::Get().Find(entry.TypeId);
        if (!info) continue;

        const void* obj = entry.GetPtrConst(reg, e);
        if (!obj) continue;

        nlohmann::json comp;
        for (const auto& prop : info->Properties)
        {
            if (prop.Transient) continue;
            comp[prop.Name] = SerializeProperty(obj, prop);
        }
        out[entry.Name] = std::move(comp);
    }
    return out;
}

void PropertySerializer::DeserializeEntity(entt::registry& reg, entt::entity e,
                                           const nlohmann::json& j)
{
    auto& cr = ComponentRegistry::Get();

    for (auto it = j.begin(); it != j.end(); ++it)
    {
        const std::string& compName = it.key();
        const auto* entry = cr.Find(compName);
        if (!entry)
        {
            BINAX_LOG_WARN("Unknown component '{}' in scene file", compName);
            continue;
        }

        if (entry->Transient)
        {
            BINAX_LOG_WARN("Skipping transient component '{}' from scene file", compName);
            continue;
        }

        if (!entry->Has(reg, e))
            entry->AddDefault(reg, e);

        void* obj = entry->GetPtr(reg, e);
        const ClassInfo* info = TypeRegistry::Get().Find(entry->TypeId);
        if (!obj || !info) continue;

        const auto& comp = it.value();
        for (const auto& prop : info->Properties)
        {
            if (prop.Transient) continue;
            if (!comp.contains(prop.Name)) continue;
            DeserializeProperty(obj, prop, comp[prop.Name]);
        }
    }
}

}

#pragma once

#include <entt/entt.hpp>
#include <nlohmann/json.hpp>
#include <string>

namespace Binax::Serialization {

class PropertySerializer
{
public:
    
    static nlohmann::json SerializeEntity(entt::registry& reg, entt::entity e);

    
    static void DeserializeEntity(entt::registry& reg, entt::entity e,
                                  const nlohmann::json& j);
};

================================================================================
FILE: Serialization/SceneSerializer.cpp
================================================================================
#include "SceneSerializer.h"
#include "Serialization/JsonHelpers.h"
#include "ECS/Components/SystemEntityTag.h"
#include "ECS/Components/Transform.h"
#include "ECS/Components/NameComponent.h"
#include "ECS/Components/MeshComponent.h"
#include "ECS/Components/MaterialComponent.h"
#include "ECS/Components/ParentComponent.h"
#include "Serialization/PropertySerializer.h"
#include "Reflect/ComponentRegistry.h"
#include "Renderer/MeshLibrary.h"
#include "Renderer/MaterialLibrary.h"
#include "Utils/Log.h"
#include "Utils/Paths.h"

#include <fstream>
#include <sstream>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <filesystem>

namespace Binax
{

using namespace Serialization::Json;




static bool ValidateSceneJson(const nlohmann::json& root)
{
    if (!root.is_object())
        return false;

    const auto version = root.find("version");
    if (version == root.end() || !version->is_number_integer())
        return false;

    const auto entities = root.find("entities");
    if (entities != root.end())
    {
        if (!entities->is_array())
            return false;
        for (const auto& ej : *entities)
            if (!ej.is_object())
                return false;
    }

    return true;
}




void SceneSerializer::Serialize(entt::registry& registry, const std::string& filepath)
{
    const std::string json = SerializeToString(registry);

    std::ofstream file(Paths::FromUtf8(filepath));
    if (!file.is_open())
    {
        BINAX_LOG_ERROR("Failed to save level to {0}", filepath);
        return;
    }

    file << json;
    file.flush();
    const bool writeOk = file.good();
    file.close();

    if (!writeOk)
    {
        BINAX_LOG_ERROR("Failed to write level to {0}", filepath);
        return;
    }


    size_t entityCount = 0;
    for (auto e : registry.storage<entt::entity>())
        if (!registry.any_of<SystemEntityTag>(e))
            ++entityCount;

    BINAX_LOG_INFO("Level saved to {0} (v{1}, {2} entities, {3} materials)",
                   filepath, kSceneVersion, entityCount,
                   MaterialLibrary::Get().GetMaterialCount());
}




std::string SceneSerializer::SerializeToString(entt::registry& registry)
{
    nlohmann::json root;
    root["version"]  = kSceneVersion;
    root["entities"] = nlohmann::json::array();

    std::vector<entt::entity> entities;
    for (auto entity : registry.storage<entt::entity>())
    {
        if (!registry.any_of<SystemEntityTag>(entity))
            entities.push_back(entity);
    }

    std::unordered_map<entt::entity, int> ids;
    ids.reserve(entities.size());
    for (size_t i = 0; i < entities.size(); ++i)
        ids[entities[i]] = static_cast<int>(i);

    int nextId = 0;
    for (auto entity : entities)
    {
        nlohmann::json entityJson;
        entityJson["id"] = nextId++;
        SerializeEntity(registry, entity, entityJson);

        if (const auto* pc = registry.try_get<ParentComponent>(entity))
        {
            const auto it = ids.find(pc->Parent);
            if (it != ids.end() && pc->Parent != entity)
                entityJson["parent"] = it->second;
        }

        root["entities"].push_back(std::move(entityJson));
    }

    return root.dump();
}




bool SceneSerializer::Deserialize(entt::registry& registry, const std::string& filepath)
{
    std::ifstream file(Paths::FromUtf8(filepath));
    if (!file.is_open())
    {
        BINAX_LOG_ERROR("Failed to open level file '{0}'", filepath);
        return false;
    }

    std::stringstream ss;
    ss << file.rdbuf();
    file.close();

    return DeserializeFromString(registry, ss.str());
}




bool SceneSerializer::DeserializeFromString(entt::registry& registry,
                                            const std::string& json_string)
{
    nlohmann::json root;
    try
    {
        root = nlohmann::json::parse(json_string);
    }
    catch (const std::exception& e)
    {
        BINAX_LOG_ERROR("Failed to parse level JSON: {0}", e.what());
        return false;
    }

    if (!ValidateSceneJson(root))
    {
        BINAX_LOG_ERROR("Level JSON has invalid structure (expected object with "
                        "integer 'version' and array of objects 'entities')");
        return false;
    }

    const int fileVersion = root["version"].get<int>();
    if (fileVersion != kSceneVersion)
    {
        BINAX_LOG_ERROR("Level version {0} != engine version {1}. "
                        "Re-save the level with the current engine.",
                        fileVersion, kSceneVersion);
        return false;
    }


    std::vector<entt::entity> toDestroy;
    for (auto e : registry.storage<entt::entity>())
    {
        if (!registry.any_of<SystemEntityTag>(e))
            toDestroy.push_back(e);
    }
    for (auto e : toDestroy)
        registry.destroy(e);

    const auto entitiesIt = root.find("entities");
    if (entitiesIt == root.end() || entitiesIt->empty())
    {
        return true;
    }

    std::vector<std::pair<entt::entity, const nlohmann::json*>> created;
    created.reserve(entitiesIt->size());

    std::unordered_map<int, entt::entity> byId;
    byId.reserve(entitiesIt->size());

    int fallbackId = 0;
    for (const auto& entityJson : *entitiesIt)
    {
        const entt::entity entity = registry.create();
        const int localId = Int(entityJson, "id", fallbackId);
        ++fallbackId;
        byId[localId] = entity;
        created.emplace_back(entity, &entityJson);
    }

    try
    {
        for (auto& [entity, entityJson] : created)
            ApplyEntityData(registry, entity, *entityJson);
    }
    catch (const std::exception& e)
    {
        BINAX_LOG_ERROR("Failed to load entities from JSON: {0}", e.what());
        return false;
    }


    for (auto& [entity, entityJson] : created)
    {
        const int parentId = Int(*entityJson, "parent", -1);
        if (parentId < 0)
            continue;

        const auto it = byId.find(parentId);
        if (it == byId.end() || it->second == entity)
            continue;

        registry.emplace_or_replace<ParentComponent>(entity,
            ParentComponent{ it->second });
    }

    return true;
}




void SceneSerializer::SerializeEntity(entt::registry& registry,
                                      entt::entity entity,
                                      nlohmann::json& outJson)
{
    outJson["components"] = Serialization::PropertySerializer::SerializeEntity(registry, entity);

    if (registry.any_of<MeshComponent>(entity))
    {
        auto h = registry.get<MeshComponent>(entity).MeshId;
        const std::string name = MeshLibrary::Get().IsValid(h)
                                     ? MeshLibrary::Get().GetName(h)
                                     : std::string{};
        outJson["components"]["Mesh Component"]["MeshName"] = name;
    }

    if (registry.any_of<MaterialComponent>(entity))
    {
        auto h = registry.get<MaterialComponent>(entity).MaterialId;
        const std::string name = MaterialLibrary::Get().IsValid(h)
                                     ? MaterialLibrary::Get().GetName(h)
                                     : std::string{};
        outJson["components"]["Material"]["MaterialName"] = name;
    }
}




void SceneSerializer::ApplyEntityData(entt::registry& registry,
                                      entt::entity entity,
                                      const nlohmann::json& json)
{
    if (json.contains("components") && json["components"].is_object())
    {
        Serialization::PropertySerializer::DeserializeEntity(registry, entity, json["components"]);

        const auto& c = json["components"];

        if (c.contains("Mesh Component") && registry.any_of<MeshComponent>(entity))
        {
            auto name = String(c["Mesh Component"], "MeshName");
            if (!name.empty())
            {
                MeshHandle h = MeshLibrary::Get().Find(name);
                registry.get<MeshComponent>(entity).MeshId = h;
                if (h == kInvalidMesh)
                    BINAX_LOG_WARN("Level: unknown mesh '{}'", name);
            }
        }

        if (c.contains("Material") && registry.any_of<MaterialComponent>(entity))
        {
            auto name = String(c["Material"], "MaterialName");
            if (!name.empty())
            {
                MaterialHandle h = MaterialLibrary::Get().Find(name);
                registry.get<MaterialComponent>(entity).MaterialId = h;
                if (h == kInvalidMaterial)
                    BINAX_LOG_WARN("Level: unknown material '{}' (.bxmat not found?)", name);
            }
        }
    }

    if (!registry.any_of<Transform>(entity))
        registry.emplace<Transform>(entity);

    if (!registry.any_of<NameComponent>(entity))
    {
        registry.emplace<NameComponent>(entity,
            "Entity " + std::to_string(static_cast<uint32_t>(entity)));
    }
}

}

================================================================================
FILE: Serialization/SceneSerializer.h
================================================================================
#pragma once

#include <entt/entt.hpp>
#include <nlohmann/json.hpp>
#include <string>

namespace Binax
{

class SceneSerializer
{
public:
    static constexpr int kSceneVersion = 4;

    static void Serialize(entt::registry& registry, const std::string& filepath);

    static bool Deserialize(entt::registry& registry, const std::string& filepath);


    static std::string SerializeToString(entt::registry& registry);
    static bool DeserializeFromString(entt::registry& registry,
                                      const std::string& json_string);

private:
    static void SerializeEntity(entt::registry& registry,
                                entt::entity entity,
                                nlohmann::json& outJson);

    static void ApplyEntityData(entt::registry& registry,
                                entt::entity entity,
                                const nlohmann::json& json);
};

}

================================================================================
FILE: Utils/FileIO.h

#pragma once

#include <cstdio>
#include <string>

#ifdef _WIN32
#  ifndef WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#    define WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#  endif
   
   
#  ifndef NOMINMAX
#    define NOMINMAX
#  endif
#  include <windows.h>
#endif

namespace Binax {




inline std::FILE* OpenFileUtf8(const std::string& pathUtf8)
{
#ifdef _WIN32
    if (pathUtf8.empty())
        return nullptr;

    const int size = MultiByteToWideChar(
        CP_UTF8, 0, pathUtf8.c_str(),
        static_cast<int>(pathUtf8.size()), nullptr, 0);
    if (size <= 0)
        return nullptr;

    std::wstring wpath(size, L'\0');
    MultiByteToWideChar(
        CP_UTF8, 0, pathUtf8.c_str(),
        static_cast<int>(pathUtf8.size()), wpath.data(), size);

    return _wfopen(wpath.c_str(), L"rb");
#else
    return std::fopen(pathUtf8.c_str(), "rb");
#endif
}

}

================================================================================
FILE: Utils/Frustum.h
================================================================================
#pragma once

#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <array>

namespace Binax {




struct Frustum
{
    
    
    
    std::array<glm::vec4, 6> Planes{};

    static Frustum FromViewProjection(const glm::mat4& viewProj);
};

inline Frustum Frustum::FromViewProjection(const glm::mat4& viewProj)
{
    
    
    const glm::vec4 r0(viewProj[0][0], viewProj[1][0], viewProj[2][0], viewProj[3][0]);
    const glm::vec4 r1(viewProj[0][1], viewProj[1][1], viewProj[2][1], viewProj[3][1]);
    const glm::vec4 r2(viewProj[0][2], viewProj[1][2], viewProj[2][2], viewProj[3][2]);
    const glm::vec4 r3(viewProj[0][3], viewProj[1][3], viewProj[2][3], viewProj[3][3]);

    Frustum f;
    f.Planes[0] = r3 + r0;   
    f.Planes[1] = r3 - r0;   
    f.Planes[2] = r3 + r1;   
    f.Planes[3] = r3 - r1;   
    f.Planes[4] = r3 + r2;   
    f.Planes[5] = r3 - r2;   

    
    
    for (glm::vec4& p : f.Planes)
    {
        const float len = glm::length(glm::vec3(p));
        if (len > 1e-8f)
            p /= len;
    }

    return f;
}

}

================================================================================
FILE: Utils/Log.cpp
================================================================================
#include "Log.h"
#include "Paths.h"
#include "Utils/LogSink.h"

#include <spdlog/sinks/basic_file_sink.h>
#include <spdlog/sinks/stdout_color_sinks.h>

#include <cstdio>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace Binax {

std::shared_ptr<spdlog::logger> Log::s_CoreLogger;

void Log::Init()
{
    if (s_CoreLogger)
        return;

    std::vector<spdlog::sink_ptr> sinks;

    auto consoleSink = std::make_shared<spdlog::sinks::stdout_color_sink_mt>();
    consoleSink->set_pattern("%^[%T] %n: %v%$");
    sinks.push_back(consoleSink);

    try
    {
        const std::filesystem::path logPath = Paths::GetExecutableDir() / "debug.log";
        auto fileSink = std::make_shared<spdlog::sinks::basic_file_sink_mt>(
            Paths::ToUtf8(logPath), true);
        fileSink->set_pattern("[%Y-%m-%d %T] [%l] %n: %v");
        sinks.push_back(fileSink);
    }
    catch (const std::exception& ex)
    {
        std::fprintf(stderr, "BINAX log: file sink init failed: %s\n", ex.what());
    }


    sinks.push_back(Binax::LogSink::Instance());

    s_CoreLogger = std::make_shared<spdlog::logger>(
        "BINAX", sinks.begin(), sinks.end());
    s_CoreLogger->set_level(spdlog::level::trace);
    s_CoreLogger->flush_on(spdlog::level::trace);
    spdlog::register_logger(s_CoreLogger);
}

}

================================================================================
FILE: Utils/Log.h
================================================================================

#pragma once

#include <spdlog/spdlog.h>
#include <spdlog/sinks/stdout_color_sinks.h>
#include <memory>

namespace Binax {

class Log
{
public:
    static void Init();

    
    
    
    static std::shared_ptr<spdlog::logger>& GetCoreLogger()
    {
        if (!s_CoreLogger)
            Init();
        return s_CoreLogger;
    }

private:
    static std::shared_ptr<spdlog::logger> s_CoreLogger;
};

} 


#define BINAX_LOG_TRACE(...)    ::Binax::Log::GetCoreLogger()->trace(__VA_ARGS__)
#define BINAX_LOG_INFO(...)     ::Binax::Log::GetCoreLogger()->info(__VA_ARGS__)
#define BINAX_LOG_WARN(...)     ::Binax::Log::GetCoreLogger()->warn(__VA_ARGS__)
#define BINAX_LOG_ERROR(...)    ::Binax::Log::GetCoreLogger()->error(__VA_ARGS__)
#define BINAX_LOG_CRITICAL(...) ::Binax::Log::GetCoreLogger()->critical(__VA_ARGS__)

================================================================================
FILE: Utils/LogSink.h
================================================================================
#pragma once

#include <spdlog/sinks/base_sink.h>
#include <spdlog/details/log_msg.h>
#include <memory>
#include <mutex>
#include <deque>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace Binax {

struct LogEntry
{
    spdlog::level::level_enum Level = spdlog::level::info;
    std::string               Message;
};

class LogSink : public spdlog::sinks::base_sink<std::mutex>
{
public:
    static std::shared_ptr<LogSink> Instance()
    {
        static std::shared_ptr<LogSink> s(new LogSink());
        return s;
    }

    std::vector<LogEntry> Snapshot()
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        return std::vector<LogEntry>(m_Entries.begin(), m_Entries.end());
    }

    void Clear()
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        m_Entries.clear();
    }

protected:
    void sink_it_(const spdlog::details::log_msg& msg) override
    {
        m_Entries.push_back({ msg.level,
            std::string(msg.payload.begin(), msg.payload.end()) });

        if (m_Entries.size() > kMaxEntries)
            m_Entries.pop_front();
    }

    void flush_() override {}

private:
    LogSink() = default;
    static constexpr size_t kMaxEntries = 8192;
    std::deque<LogEntry> m_Entries;
};

}

================================================================================
FILE: Utils/Paths.h
================================================================================
#pragma once

#include <filesystem>
#include <string>
#include <system_error>

#ifdef _WIN32
#  ifndef WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#    define WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#  endif
#  ifndef NOMINMAX
#    define NOMINMAX
#  endif
#  include <windows.h>
#else
#  include <limits.h>
#  include <unistd.h>
#endif

namespace Binax::Paths {




inline std::string ToUtf8(const std::filesystem::path& p)
{
    const auto u8 = p.u8string();
    return std::string(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(u8.data()), u8.size());
}

inline std::filesystem::path FromUtf8(const std::string& s)
{
    return std::filesystem::path(
        std::u8string(reinterpret_cast<const char8_t*>(s.data()), s.size()));
}


inline std::filesystem::path GetExecutableDir()
{
#ifdef _WIN32
    
    wchar_t buf[32768];
    const DWORD n = GetModuleFileNameW(nullptr, buf,
                                       static_cast<DWORD>(sizeof(buf) / sizeof(buf[0])));
    if (n == 0 || n >= sizeof(buf) / sizeof(buf[0]))
        return {};
    return std::filesystem::path(std::wstring(buf, n)).parent_path();
#else
    char buf[PATH_MAX];
    const ssize_t n = readlink("/proc/self/exe", buf, sizeof(buf) - 1);
    if (n <= 0)
        return {};
    buf[n] = '\0';
    return std::filesystem::path(std::string(buf)).parent_path();
#endif
}









inline std::string Resolve(const std::string& relativeOrAbsolute)
{
    if (relativeOrAbsolute.empty())
        return relativeOrAbsolute;

    std::error_code ec;
    const auto asGiven = FromUtf8(relativeOrAbsolute);
    if (std::filesystem::exists(asGiven, ec) && !ec)
        return relativeOrAbsolute;

    const auto exeDir = GetExecutableDir();
    if (!exeDir.empty())
    {
        const auto candidate = exeDir / asGiven;
        if (std::filesystem::exists(candidate, ec) && !ec)
            return ToUtf8(candidate);
    }

    return relativeOrAbsolute;
}

}

namespace {

entt::entity CreateEditorCameraEntity(entt::registry& registry, float aspect)
{
    auto e = registry.create();
    registry.emplace<Binax::NameComponent>(e, "EditorCamera");
    registry.emplace<Binax::Transform>(e,
                                       glm::vec3(0.0f, 1.0f, 3.0f),
                                       glm::quat(1.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f),
                                       glm::vec3(1.0f));
    registry.emplace<Binax::EditorCameraComponent>(e);
    registry.emplace<Binax::CameraComponent>(e,
                                             Binax::ProjectionType::Perspective,
                                             45.0f, 0.1f, 100.0f, 5.0f,
                                             aspect,
                                             true);
    registry.emplace<Binax::SystemEntityTag>(e);
    return e;
}

entt::entity CreateSkyboxEntity(entt::registry& registry)
{
    auto e = registry.create();
    registry.emplace<Binax::NameComponent>(e, "Skybox");
    registry.emplace<Binax::SkyboxComponent>(e);
    return e;
}

void EnsureSkyboxExists(entt::registry& registry)
{
    auto view = registry.view<Binax::SkyboxComponent>();
    if (view.begin() == view.end())
        CreateSkyboxEntity(registry);
}

entt::entity CreatePostProcessCfgEntity(entt::registry& registry)
{
    auto e = registry.create();
    registry.emplace<Binax::NameComponent>(e, "PostProcessingCfg");
    registry.emplace<Binax::Transform>(e);
    registry.emplace<Binax::PostProcessComponent>(e);
    registry.emplace<Binax::SystemEntityTag>(e);
    return e;
}

void EnsurePostProcessCfgExists(entt::registry& registry)
{
    auto view = registry.view<Binax::PostProcessComponent>();
    if (view.begin() == view.end())
        CreatePostProcessCfgEntity(registry);
}

Binax::PostProcessSettings GatherPostProcessSettings(entt::registry& registry)
{
    Binax::PostProcessSettings s;

    s.TonemapMode = static_cast<int>(Binax::TonemapMode::Linear);

    auto view = registry.view<Binax::PostProcessComponent>();
    for (auto e : view)
    {
        const auto& c = view.get<Binax::PostProcessComponent>(e);
        if (!c.Enabled)
            return s;

        s.Enabled  = true;
        s.Exposure = c.Exposure;

        s.TonemapMode = static_cast<int>(c.Tonemap);
        s.Contrast    = c.Contrast;
        s.Saturation  = c.Saturation;
        s.Temperature = c.Temperature;
        s.Tint        = c.Tint;
        s.Lift        = c.Lift;
        s.Gamma       = c.Gamma;
        s.Gain        = c.Gain;

        s.BloomEnabled   = c.BloomEnabled;
        s.BloomThreshold = c.BloomThreshold;
        s.BloomIntensity = c.BloomIntensity;

        s.MotionBlurEnabled  = c.MotionBlurEnabled;
        s.MotionBlurStrength = c.MotionBlurStrength;
        s.MotionBlurSamples  = c.MotionBlurSamples;

        s.LensDistortionEnabled = c.LensDistortionEnabled;
        s.LensDistortion        = c.LensDistortion;

        s.ChromaticAberrationEnabled = c.ChromaticAberrationEnabled;
        s.ChromaticAberration        = c.ChromaticAberration;

        s.VignetteEnabled    = c.VignetteEnabled;
        s.VignetteIntensity  = c.VignetteIntensity;
        s.VignetteSmoothness = c.VignetteSmoothness;

        s.FilmGrainEnabled   = c.FilmGrainEnabled;
        s.FilmGrainIntensity = c.FilmGrainIntensity;

        s.FogEnabled = c.FogEnabled;
        s.FogMode    = static_cast<int>(c.Fog);
        s.FogColor   = c.FogColor;
        s.FogDensity = c.FogDensity;
        s.FogStart   = c.FogStart;
        s.FogEnd     = c.FogEnd;

        return s;
    }

    return s;
}

Binax::SkyboxParams GatherSkyboxParams(entt::registry& registry)
{
    Binax::SkyboxParams params;

    auto view = registry.view<Binax::SkyboxComponent>();
    for (auto e : view)
    {
        auto& sc = view.get<Binax::SkyboxComponent>(e);

        if (!sc.Enabled) return params;

        std::array<std::string, 6> faces = {
            sc.FacePX, sc.FaceNX,
            sc.FacePY, sc.FaceNY,
            sc.FacePZ, sc.FaceNZ
        };

        bool complete = true;
        for (const auto& p : faces)
            if (p.empty()) { complete = false; break; }
        if (!complete) return params;

        params.CubemapPtr      = Binax::AssetRegistry::Get().GetCubemap(faces, true);
        params.RotationRadians = glm::radians(sc.Rotation);
        params.Brightness      = sc.Brightness;
        return params;
    }
    return params;
}

} 

int main()
{
#ifdef _WIN32
    if (HWND hwnd = GetConsoleWindow())
        ShowWindow(hwnd, SW_HIDE);
#endif

    Binax::Log::Init();
    Binax::Reflect::RegisterEngineComponents();
    BINAX_LOG_INFO("Binax Engine Starting...");

    int exitCode = 0;

    try
    {
        Binax::Window::Props props;
        props.Title  = "Binax Engine";
        props.Width  = 1920;
        props.Height = 1080;
        props.VSync  = true;

        Binax::Window window(props);
        Binax::Input::SetWindow(window.GetGLFWWindow());

        if (!gladLoadGLLoader((GLADloadproc)glfwGetProcAddress))
        {
            BINAX_LOG_ERROR("Failed to initialize GLAD");
            throw std::runtime_error("Failed to initialize GLAD");
        }

        BINAX_LOG_INFO("OpenGL: {0}",
            reinterpret_cast<const char*>(glGetString(GL_VERSION)));

        if (!GLAD_GL_ARB_bindless_texture)
        {
            BINAX_LOG_ERROR("GL_ARB_bindless_texture required but not available");
            throw std::runtime_error("Missing required extension: GL_ARB_bindless_texture");
        }
        BINAX_LOG_INFO("ARB_bindless_texture: available");

        if (!GLAD_GL_ARB_shader_draw_parameters)
        {
            BINAX_LOG_ERROR("GL_ARB_shader_draw_parameters required but not available");
            throw std::runtime_error("Missing required extension: GL_ARB_shader_draw_parameters");
        }
        BINAX_LOG_INFO("ARB_shader_draw_parameters: available");

        {
            GLint major = 0, minor = 0;
            glGetIntegerv(GL_MAJOR_VERSION, &major);
            glGetIntegerv(GL_MINOR_VERSION, &minor);
            BINAX_LOG_INFO("Context version: {}.{} (bindless handles as uvec2)",
                           major, minor);
        }

        Binax::Timer timer;
        Binax::DeferredRenderer renderer(props.Width, props.Height);

        {
            auto cube = Binax::Mesh::CreateCube();
            Binax::MeshLibrary::Get().Add("Cube", cube.Vertices, cube.Indices);

            auto quad = Binax::Mesh::CreateFullscreenQuad();
            Binax::MeshLibrary::Get().Add("FullscreenQuad", quad.Vertices, quad.Indices);

            Binax::MeshLibrary::Get().Flush();
            BINAX_LOG_INFO("Built-in meshes registered");
        }

        Binax::MaterialSerializer::LoadAll(
            Binax::MaterialLibrary::Get(),
            Binax::Paths::Resolve(Binax::AssetFiles::kMaterialsDir));

        entt::registry registry;

        CreateEditorCameraEntity(registry, window.GetAspect());
        BINAX_LOG_INFO("EditorCamera created");

        EnsureSkyboxExists(registry);
        BINAX_LOG_INFO("Skybox entity ensured");

        EnsurePostProcessCfgExists(registry);
        BINAX_LOG_INFO("PostProcessingCfg entity ensured");

        {
            const std::string ppPath =
                Binax::Paths::Resolve(Binax::AssetFiles::kPostProcessFile);

            std::error_code ec;
            if (std::filesystem::exists(Binax::Paths::FromUtf8(ppPath), ec) && !ec)
            {
                if (!Binax::PostProcessSerializer::Load(registry, ppPath))
                    BINAX_LOG_WARN("Post-processing load failed — using defaults");
            }
            else
            {
                BINAX_LOG_INFO("No {} found — using default post-processing",
                               Binax::AssetFiles::kPostProcessFile);
            }
        }

        {
            const std::string levelPath =
                Binax::Paths::Resolve(Binax::AssetFiles::kLevelFile);

            std::error_code ec;
            const bool levelExists =
                std::filesystem::exists(Binax::Paths::FromUtf8(levelPath), ec) && !ec;

            if (levelExists)
            {
                if (Binax::SceneSerializer::Deserialize(registry, levelPath))
                    BINAX_LOG_INFO("Level loaded from disk");
                else
                    BINAX_LOG_WARN("Level load failed — starting empty");
            }
            else
            {
                BINAX_LOG_INFO("No {} found — starting empty", Binax::AssetFiles::kLevelFile);
            }
        }

        EnsureSkyboxExists(registry);
        EnsurePostProcessCfgExists(registry);

        Binax::Viewport viewport(props.Width, props.Height);
        Binax::EditorCamera editorCamera(viewport.GetAspect());
        Binax::EditorUI editorUI(window.GetGLFWWindow(), registry);
        editorUI.SetViewport(&viewport);



        Binax::FrameRenderData frameData;


        editorUI.GetShaderEditor().SetReloadCallback(
            [&renderer](const std::string& )
            {
                renderer.ReloadShaders();
            });


        editorUI.SetPickCallback(
            [&](ImVec2 uv) -> entt::entity
            {
                return Binax::RenderSystem::PickEntityAtUV(
                    frameData,
                    renderer.GetGBuffer(),
                    uv.x, uv.y);
            });

        bool vsyncEnabled = props.VSync;
        float smoothedFps = 0.0f;









        editorUI.AddPanelDrawer([&]()
        {
            const bool wantOverlay =
                editorUI.IsViewportStatsVisible() && editorUI.HasViewport();

            if (wantOverlay)
            {
                const ImVec4 r = editorUI.GetViewportScreenRect();



                ImGui::SetNextWindowPos(
                    ImVec2(r.x + r.z - 290.0f, r.y + 8.0f),
                    ImGuiCond_Always);


                ImGui::SetNextWindowBgAlpha(0.92f);
            }

            ImGui::SetNextWindowSize(ImVec2(280.0f, 420.0f),
                                     ImGuiCond_FirstUseEver);

            bool open = true;
            ImGui::Begin("Stats", &open, ImGuiWindowFlags_NoCollapse);

            if (wantOverlay)
            {


                ImGui::BringWindowToDisplayFront(ImGui::GetCurrentWindow());
            }

            const float dt = timer.GetDelta();
            const float ms = dt * 1000.0f;
            const float instFps = (dt > 1e-6f) ? (1.0f / dt) : 0.0f;

            smoothedFps = (smoothedFps <= 0.0f)
                              ? instFps
                              : smoothedFps * 0.9f + instFps * 0.1f;


            const float displayFps = iam_tween_float(
                ImGui::GetID("fps_display"),
                ImHashStr("v"),
                smoothedFps,
                0.4f,
                iam_ease_preset(iam_ease_out_cubic),
                iam_policy_crossfade,
                dt);

            ImGui::Text("FPS:        %.1f", displayFps);
            ImGui::Text("Frame time: %.2f ms", ms);


            static float fpsHistory[60] = {};
            static int   fpsOffset = 0;
            fpsHistory[fpsOffset] = smoothedFps;
            fpsOffset = (fpsOffset + 1) % IM_ARRAYSIZE(fpsHistory);
            ImGui::PlotLines("##fps", fpsHistory, IM_ARRAYSIZE(fpsHistory), fpsOffset,
                             nullptr, 0.0f, 240.0f, ImVec2(-1, 40));


            const float frameBudget = 16.6f;
            const float currentMs = 1000.0f / std::max(1.0f, smoothedFps);
            const float budget = std::clamp(currentMs / frameBudget, 0.0f, 1.0f);

            const ImU32 barCol = (budget < 0.7f) ? IM_COL32(76, 200, 100, 255)
                               : (budget < 1.0f) ? IM_COL32(255, 200, 60, 255)
                                                 : IM_COL32(230, 70, 70, 255);
            ImGui::PushStyleColor(ImGuiCol_PlotHistogram, barCol);
            ImGui::ProgressBar(budget, ImVec2(-1, 6), "");
            ImGui::PopStyleColor();

            ImGui::Separator();
            if (ImGui::Checkbox("VSync", &vsyncEnabled))
                window.SetVSync(vsyncEnabled);

            bool gpuCull = renderer.IsFrustumCullingEnabled();
            if (ImGui::Checkbox("GPU Frustum Culling", &gpuCull))
                renderer.SetFrustumCullingEnabled(gpuCull);
            if (!renderer.IsFrustumCullingAvailable())
                ImGui::TextDisabled("  (compute shader unavailable)");

            ImGui::Separator();
            const Binax::FrameStats& s = renderer.GetFrameStats();
            ImGui::Text("Draw calls: %u", s.DrawCalls);
            ImGui::Text("Vertices:   %u", s.Vertices);
            ImGui::Text("Triangles:  %u", s.Triangles);

            if (s.GpuCulling)
            {
                const uint32_t total = Binax::InstanceBuffer::Get().GetCount();
                ImGui::Text("Visible:    %u (GPU)", s.VisibleInstances);
                ImGui::Text("Culled:     %u",
                            total > s.VisibleInstances ? total - s.VisibleInstances : 0u);
            }

            ImGui::Text("TAA:        %s", s.TaaActive ? "on" : "off");
            ImGui::Text("PostFX:     %s", s.PostFXActive ? "on" : "off");

            ImGui::Separator();
            ImGui::Text("Meshes:     %u", Binax::MeshLibrary::Get().GetMeshCount());
            ImGui::Text("Materials:  %u", Binax::MaterialLibrary::Get().GetMaterialCount());
            ImGui::Text("Instances:  %u", Binax::InstanceBuffer::Get().GetCount());

            ImGui::End();


            if (!open)
                editorUI.SetViewportStatsVisible(false);
        });

        std::vector<Binax::GPULight> collectedLights;
        collectedLights.reserve(Binax::kMaxLights);
        glClearColor(0.08f, 0.08f, 0.10f, 1.0f);




        while (!window.ShouldClose())
        {
            timer.Update();
            float delta = timer.GetDelta();


            iam_update_begin_frame();
            iam_clip_update(delta);


            Binax::Input::Update();
            window.Update();

            editorUI.SyncViewportSize();

            const float vpAspect = viewport.GetAspect();
            editorCamera.OnResize(vpAspect);

            {
                glm::vec2 vpSize = viewport.GetSize();
                uint32_t vpW = (uint32_t)vpSize.x;
                uint32_t vpH = (uint32_t)vpSize.y;
                if (vpW > 0 && vpH > 0 &&
                    (vpW != renderer.GetGBuffer().GetWidth() ||
                     vpH != renderer.GetGBuffer().GetHeight()))
                {
                    renderer.Resize(vpW, vpH);
                }
            }

            if (Binax::Input::IsKeyPressed(GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE) &&
                !ImGui::GetIO().WantTextInput)
                window.SetShouldClose(true);

            auto cameraView = registry.view<Binax::Transform,
                                            Binax::EditorCameraComponent,
                                            Binax::CameraComponent>();

            if (cameraView.begin() == cameraView.end())
            {
                BINAX_LOG_ERROR("EditorCamera missing! Recreating...");
                CreateEditorCameraEntity(registry, vpAspect);
                cameraView = registry.view<Binax::Transform,
                                           Binax::EditorCameraComponent,
                                           Binax::CameraComponent>();
            }

            Binax::FXAAParams frameFXAA{};
            Binax::TAAParams  frameTAA{};

            for (auto entity : cameraView)
            {
                auto& transform     = cameraView.get<Binax::Transform>(entity);
                auto& editorCamComp = cameraView.get<Binax::EditorCameraComponent>(entity);
                auto& camComp       = cameraView.get<Binax::CameraComponent>(entity);

                camComp.AspectRatio = vpAspect;

                editorCamera.SetPosition(transform.Position);
                editorCamera.SetOrientation(transform.Rotation);
                editorCamera.SetFOV(camComp.FOV);
                editorCamera.SetNearFar(camComp.NearPlane, camComp.FarPlane);
                editorCamera.SetMoveSpeed(editorCamComp.MoveSpeed);
                editorCamera.SetRotationSpeed(editorCamComp.RotationSpeed);
                editorCamera.SetSpeedMultiplier(editorCamComp.SpeedMultiplier);

                frameFXAA.Enabled          = camComp.FXAAEnabled;
                frameFXAA.EdgeThreshold    = camComp.FXAAEdgeThreshold;
                frameFXAA.EdgeThresholdMin = camComp.FXAAEdgeThresholdMin;
                frameFXAA.SubpixelBlending = camComp.FXAASubpixelBlending;

                frameTAA.Enabled       = camComp.TAAEnabled;
                frameTAA.HistoryWeight = camComp.TAAHistoryWeight;

                break;
            }

            editorCamera.SetMouseOver(editorUI.IsViewportHovered());
            editorCamera.SetGizmoActive(editorUI.IsGizmoUsing());
            editorCamera.Update(delta);

            for (auto entity : cameraView)
            {
                auto& transform = cameraView.get<Binax::Transform>(entity);
                transform.Position = editorCamera.GetPosition();
                transform.Rotation = editorCamera.GetOrientation();
                break;
            }

            editorUI.SetCameraMatrices(editorCamera.GetViewMatrix(),
                                       editorCamera.GetProjectionMatrix());

            renderer.SetTAAEnabled(frameTAA.Enabled);
            renderer.ClearGBuffer();

            Binax::TransformSystem::UpdateWorldTransforms(registry);

            Binax::RenderSystem::BuildFrameData(registry, frameData);

    renderer.GeometryPass(frameData,
                          editorCamera.GetViewMatrix(),
                          editorCamera.GetProjectionMatrix());


    {
        const auto& selSet = editorUI.GetSelectedEntities();
        std::vector<entt::entity> selVec(selSet.begin(), selSet.end());
        renderer.RenderOutlinePass(
            frameData,
            selVec,
            editorCamera.GetViewMatrix(),
            editorCamera.GetProjectionMatrix(),
            (uint32_t)viewport.GetSize().x,
            (uint32_t)viewport.GetSize().y);
    }


    collectedLights.clear();
            Binax::RenderSystem::CollectLights(registry, collectedLights);

            Binax::SkyboxParams skyboxParams = GatherSkyboxParams(registry);

            const Binax::PostProcessSettings postSettings =
                GatherPostProcessSettings(registry);

            renderer.LightingPass(collectedLights,
                                  editorCamera.GetPosition(),
                                  editorCamera.GetViewMatrix(),
                                  editorCamera.GetProjectionMatrix(),
                                  skyboxParams,
                                  viewport.GetFBO(),
                                  (uint32_t)viewport.GetSize().x,
                                  (uint32_t)viewport.GetSize().y,
                                  frameFXAA,
                                  frameTAA,
                                  postSettings,
                                  timer.GetElapsed());

            glBindFramebuffer(GL_FRAMEBUFFER, 0);
            glViewport(0, 0, window.GetWidth(), window.GetHeight());
            glClear(GL_COLOR_BUFFER_BIT);

            editorUI.Draw();


            static float animGcTimer = 0.0f;
            if ((animGcTimer += delta) > 1.0f) {
                iam_gc(600);
                iam_clip_gc(600);
                animGcTimer = 0.0f;
            }


            window.SwapBuffers();
        }

        BINAX_LOG_INFO("Shutting down...");

        Binax::InstanceBuffer::Get().Clear();
        Binax::AssetRegistry::Get().Clear();
        Binax::MaterialLibrary::Get().Clear();
        Binax::MeshLibrary::Get().Clear();

        iam_clip_shutdown();
    }
    catch (const std::exception& e)
    {
        BINAX_LOG_ERROR("Exception: {0}", e.what());
        exitCode = 1;
    }

    glfwTerminate();
    return exitCode;
}
